# v4.4 — Council Workspace Implementation Plan

> [!IMPORTANT]
> **DE-ROTTED 2026-07-25 against main `d5b4e0b` (v4.3.0 shipped). Do NOT re-run the de-rot.**
> Findings ledger: `.superpowers/sdd/v44/preflight-v44-findings.md` (run `wf_60132c0e-90f` — 217
> agents, 209 candidates → 90 root findings → 68 confirmed, 22 refuted). Every correction is inlined
> below as a `⚠️ DE-ROT` annotation; finding ids (`F01`…`F68`) cross-reference the ledger.
>
> **Regression baseline for every task:** 397 suites / 4,764 passed + 7 skipped / 0 failed · lint
> clean · `check-file-sizes.js --all` clean · MCP tool count 16.
>
> **What the rot turned out to be.** As in v4.1 and v4.3, predictions about **exports and signatures
> held** (every Task 0 go/no-go check passes on current main). Predictions about **which file owns a
> behavior, what shape the data has, and what CI demands did not.** Three things moved under this
> plan that it could not see:
> 1. The **council** live doc is a different document from the **wave** live doc, built in
>    `src/mcp-council-awareness.js` — a file this plan never named — and it emits **no per-leg rows
>    at all** (F01). Task 0.5 below fixes that before anything consumes it.
> 2. **v4.1 debate** and **v4.2 local providers** reshaped the artifacts the GUI renders (extra
>    `runStats` rows per model, re-vote prose outside the allowlist, tier overrides in a different
>    file than the matrix reads).
> 3. **CI grew an `integration` job on 2026-07-21 — two days after this plan was written** — and
>    `docs/publishing.md` made two integration rails mandatory release gates (F06, F50).
>
> Task 0 still runs: it re-verifies the anchors and records the baseline. It is no longer where the
> rot gets discovered.

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** An Electron **Council Workspace** — third shell mode `AMICUS_MODE=council-workspace`, entered via `amicus watch <councilRunId> --ui` — that renders any council run (live or historical) in one window: run list, stage rail, live seats, anonymized Stage-2 packets, adjudication matrix, dissent drill-in, chair verdict, cost-by-seat with `--max-cost` gauge, plus exactly two verbs (confirm-gated Abort, nonced Fold) on a strictly read-only, fully sandboxed first-party page.

> ⚠️ **DE-ROT (F01, architecture amendment):** "never new plumbing" is now false in exactly one
> place, by explicit decision. The council composed live doc emits no per-leg rows, so the live
> Seats panel has no source (see F01). **Task 0.5** adds them in
> `src/mcp-council-awareness.js:buildCouncilStatusPayload` before any GUI code consumes them. That
> is one additive, tested change to the v4.3 data layer; everything downstream of it remains pixels
> over existing data. Read the architecture sentence as: *the GUI adds no plumbing of its own — the
> one datum it needs and cannot get is added to the data layer that owns it, not to the GUI.*

**Architecture:** The GUI is pixels over existing data, never new plumbing (spec §2): historical runs render purely from the v4.0 run directory (run.json / tally.json / verdict.json / prose artifacts) resolved through the sessions-dir pointer files; live runs add a renderer-driven 1.5–2 s poll of the v4.3 composed live doc (`handlers.amicus_status` called in-process — the exact `src/cli-handlers-status.js:62` precedent). All filesystem reads happen in the Electron **main** process behind a 7-channel `workspace:` IPC surface (sender-pinned via `electron/ipc-guard.js`); the renderer is `sandbox: true` + `contextIsolation` + CSP, loads static files via `loadFile` (a real `file://` origin, so `contextBridge` works under full sandbox — unlike the toolbar's `data:` URL), and puts every model-derived string into the DOM via `textContent` only (H9 surface). Heavy pure logic (run scan, defensive parse, artifact allowlist, matrix model, blind pairs, fold text, live normalization) lives in `src/workspace/` where it is trivially unit-testable headless; the renderer keeps only paint + poll.

**Tech Stack:** Node ≥ 22.12 CommonJS, Electron 43 (already vendored/lazily provisioned via `ensureElectron()`), jest, CDP e2e via `tests/helpers/cdp-client.js`. **Zero new runtime dependencies. Zero new devDependencies.**

## Ambiguities & drift resolved while planning (visible decisions)

1. **Neutral matrix symbol — spec drift.** Spec §5.2 says "report.js `SYMBOL` ✓/✗/○", but the actual `src/council/report.js:16` is `SYMBOL = { agree: '✓', dispute: '✗', neutral: '–' }`. The plan treats the **code** as the single source: `src/workspace/matrix-model.js` `require`s `SYMBOL` from `../council/report` — whatever it says renders (today: `–` for neutral).
2. **Fold re-entry semantics.** The fold nonce is per-window-launch, so the workspace performs **one fold write per launch**: in-flight second click → `{ok:false, error:'fold-in-flight'}`; after a completed fold every further `workspace:fold` returns `{ok:true, already:true}` and the button reads "Folded ✓". The window does **not** auto-close after folding (viewer semantics; exit 0 on fold-then-close and on plain close, spec §7). Reopen the workspace to fold again (fresh nonce).
3. **Window-creation placement + basename collision.** The BrowserWindow construction is extracted to a new **`electron/workspace-shell.js`** (not left inline in the grandfathered 532-line `main.js`, and *not* named `workspace-window.js` because spec §4.3 already assigns that basename to the **CLI launcher** `src/sidecar/workspace-window.js`). `main.js` gains only a ~15-line mode branch.
4. **Where pure logic lives.** Matrix model, blind pairs, allowlist, fold text, and live normalization are built **main-process-side** in `src/workspace/` and shipped display-ready over IPC with **dual names** (`{model, label}`) on every name-bearing field — the blind toggle is then a pure client-side display flip with zero re-fetch, and `SYMBOL`/`formatCost` stay single-sourced (`src/council/report.js`, `src/utils/pricing.js`). The renderer's own pure pieces (`md-lite.js`, `live-model.js`) carry `module.exports` guards so jest (node env) tests them without a DOM.
5. **Run-list sort key.** run.json has no top-level `startedAt` in the v4.0 field list; `run-scan` uses `createdAt || stages[0].startedAt || pointer-file mtime`, descending.
6. **The A1 "in-process snapshot API" concretely** = `require('../src/mcp-server').handlers.amicus_status({taskId: runId}, project)` — requiring mcp-server does NOT start the server (documented at `src/cli-handlers-status.js:3-6`); v4.3 stamps `view:'live'` + per-leg `usage` onto that same composed doc (v4.3 §4.3). The composed-doc **shape** risk is quarantined in ONE pure function, `src/workspace/live-normalize.js` — if the merged v4.3 shape differs, that seam moves and nothing else does.

   > ⚠️ **DE-ROT (F01/F08/F03).** The call is right; the picture of what comes back is wrong.
   > `handlers.amicus_status({taskId: runId})` on a **council** id does not return the wave rollup —
   > `src/mcp-server.js:577` delegates to `require('./mcp-council-run').buildCouncilStatusPayload`,
   > implemented at `src/mcp-council-awareness.js:104-156`. Shipped shape (see
   > `schemas/council-run-live.schema.json`, asserted in `tests/schemas-live.test.js:131-170`):
   >
   > ```
   > {taskId, type:'council-run', runId, runDir, status, currentStage,
   >  stages:[{name, status, waveId}], legsTotal, legsComplete, elapsed,
   >  exitCode, version, view:'live', usage?, reason?}
   > ```
   >
   > Differences that matter: **no `legs[]`** (F01 — Task 0.5 adds it), **no `stalled`/
   > `stalledForSeconds`/`crashed`** (F03 — council crash detection flips `status` to `'error'` and
   > sets `reason` instead), stage entries carry **no timestamps** (F40), and `usage` is the
   > **active stage's** rollup rather than the run total (F39). `src/observe/live-doc.js:8` carries a
   > shipped comment saying all of this — read it first. The "quarantined in one seam" claim only
   > holds once Task 0.5 lands; without it the seam has nothing to normalize.
7. **Stage rail is generic.** It renders `run.json stages[]` in recorded order with friendly labels for known names (`stage1`, `stage2`, `tally-provisional`, `debate-defense`, `debate-revote`, `chair`, `tally` / `tally-final`, `verdict`) and raw-name passthrough for unknown ones — which is exactly how v4.1 debate stages "render gracefully when absent" (and gracefully when present).

   > ⚠️ **DE-ROT (F10) — `repairs` is a phantom stage, and four real stage names were missing.**
   > The list above is corrected in place (it read `stage1`, `repairs`, `stage2`, `tally`, `chair`,
   > `verdict`). **No stage named `repairs` is ever written** — `grep -rn "'repairs'" src/` returns
   > nothing; repair waves are appended to the OWNING stage's `waveIds` via `appendStageWave`
   > (`src/council/run-stages.js:125-126`, `:203-204`), never recorded as a stage of their own. The
   > complete shipped set is `stage1` (`run.js:126`), `stage2` (:165), `tally-provisional` (:200),
   > `debate-defense` (:205), `debate-revote` (:223), `chair` (`run-chair.js:89,92`), then `tally`
   > **or** `tally-final` — the spelling is conditional on `--debate` (`run.js:284`,
   > `const tallyStage = o.debate ? 'tally-final' : 'tally'`) — and `verdict` (:289). Keep **both**
   > tally spellings in the friendly-label map. If repair activity should be visible, surface it as
   > a sub-wave count on the owning stage (from `stage.waveIds`), never as its own rail entry.
8. **Council-aware abort delegation** = `handlers.amicus_abort({taskId: runId}, project)` in-process (v4.0 §8 wired council-awareness into `amicus_abort` via the pointer files). Zero new mutation logic; renderer confirm-gates it.
9. **Blind default is computed once per run-open** — ON when the run's status is non-terminal at open, OFF when terminal — and then only the user's toggle changes it (names must not swap mid-read when a live run completes under the viewer). Per-window, never persisted.
10. **E2E CDP port.** `docs/electron-testing.md` recommends 9223 for manual runs and the toolbar e2e suite occupies 9224; the new workspace e2e suite uses **9225** to allow side-by-side runs.
11. **`check:sizes` already covers `electron/**`** (`scripts/check-file-sizes.js` CONFIG.include = `['src/**/*.js', 'electron/**/*.js']`) — no gate change needed; the rule enforced by this plan is **grandfather nothing new** (the `exclude` list is not touched; every new file stays < 300 lines).
12. **`--ui` is interactive-only:** `watch --ui --json` fails fast with a stderr hint (documented envelope exclusion alongside `setup`/`update`/`key`, spec §2).
13. **Artifact filenames.** `review-<model>.md`/`judge-<model>.md` names are derived with v4.0's `sanitizeName()` (exported from `src/council/run-launch.js`, Plan B Task: `String(model).replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9._-]/g, '-')`) over `run.json bench` — the allowlist can therefore never contain a path separator, and realpath containment is the second, independent fence.

## Prerequisites & re-grounding

**Hard ordering: v4.0 AND v4.3 must be MERGED to main before any task below executes.**

- **v4.0 (headless council engine)** is a hard dependency for EVERYTHING: the run directory, pointer files, tally/verdict shapes, council-aware `amicus_abort`, `stripFoldMarkers`, `sanitizeName`. Historical rendering (Phases 1–2, Tasks 1–13) depends **only** on v4.0.
- **v4.3 (observability data layer)** is a hard dependency for the LIVE panels only (Phases 3+, Tasks 14–15): the composed live doc with `view:'live'` + per-leg `usage`, staleness flags, and the `watch` verb that `--ui` rides. Phases 1–2 ship value even if v4.3 slips (spec §11 phase 2 note) — but this plan's Phase 4 Task 17 wires `watch --ui`, so the **release** (Task 20) requires both merged.
- Execution starts with **Task 0**, which re-verifies every consumed interface and every file anchor against then-current main. All `file:line` references in this plan were read on 2026-07-19 at main `76758e4` (v3.2.3 + finalized 4.x specs; v4.0/v4.3 not yet merged) — **they are starting points for re-verification, not facts**.

## Global Constraints

- **Baseline for execution:** then-current main with v4.0 AND v4.3 merged (Task 0 verifies). Authoritative spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-19-v4.4-council-workspace-design.md` (finalized; all Resolved-questions LOCKED). Data contracts: `.../2026-07-19-v4.3-observability-data-layer-design.md` §4, `.../2026-07-19-v4.0-headless-council-engine-design.md` §4/§6.
- **Read-only invariant (spec §6.2):** the workspace process NEVER writes into a run directory — **except `workspace:abort-run`**. No other IPC channel has write semantics against runDir; fold writes to stdout. Any task that would violate this is a bug.

  > ⚠️ **DE-ROT (F44) — "abort delegates to the engine's own path" is misleading.** There is no
  > separate engine process to delegate to: `handlers.amicus_abort` runs **in-process**, so the
  > Electron main process itself performs the run-directory writes (`abortCouncilRun` →
  > `runState.checkpoint`, `src/mcp-council-awareness.js:206-227`). The *logic* is reused — v4.4 adds
  > no new mutation code — but the *writer* is this process. Two consequences: (1) any "the workspace
  > never writes to runDir" guard test must explicitly exclude the abort channel or it will fail
  > honestly; (2) `workspace:get-live` can also write in one case — `amicus_status` checkpoints a
  > crashed run to `'error'` as part of the engine's own crash detection. Document both rather than
  > claiming a purity the code does not have.
- **H9 renderer posture (spec §4.2):** `sandbox: true` + `contextIsolation: true` + `nodeIntegration: false`; CSP exactly `default-src 'none'; script-src file:; style-src file: 'unsafe-inline'; font-src file:; img-src file: data:`; `will-navigate` → always `preventDefault()`; `setWindowOpenHandler` → deny; **no `innerHTML` anywhere in `electron/workspace-ui/`** — model text enters the DOM via `textContent`/`createTextNode` only; zero inline `<script>` in index.html.
- **IPC surface is exactly the 7 spec channels** (`workspace:list-runs|get-run|get-live|read-artifact|abort-run|fold|open-report`); `workspace:live-update` stays reserved/unregistered. Every handler validates the sender with `isPrivilegedSender`.
- **Design system:** every color/font/spacing in `workspace.css` comes from `src/design/tokens.css` vars — **no new hex anywhere** (design-system plan Global Constraint). Dark-only. Status never by color alone (chips carry text; matrix cells carry symbols with `title` + `aria-label`). `prefers-reduced-motion` disables the running pulse. `--focus-ring` focus-visible styles; Esc dismisses dialogs.
- **300-line gate:** `node scripts/check-file-sizes.js --all` passes after every task; the `exclude` list in `scripts/check-file-sizes.js` gains **zero** new entries (`electron/main.js` is already grandfathered and its edit stays ~15 lines).

## Shared contracts (referenced by every task)

**Env contract (launcher → Electron main):** `AMICUS_MODE=council-workspace`, `AMICUS_PROJECT=<absolute project dir>`, `AMICUS_RUN_ID=<runId|''>` (empty → run-list landing), `AMICUS_FOLD_NONCE=<16-hex per launch>`; `AMICUS_DEBUG_PORT` / `AMICUS_HEADLESS_TEST` pass through via `...process.env`. Direct-launch with `AMICUS_RUN_ID` set opens straight into that run's detail (spec §5).

**IPC surface (spec §4.5, exact):**

| Channel | Args → Returns |
|---|---|
| `workspace:list-runs` | `()` → `RunRow[]` (error rows inline, never throws) |
| `workspace:get-run` | `(runId)` → `RunDetail` |
| `workspace:get-live` | `(runId)` → `LiveModel` (normalized; `{ok:false, error}` on failure) |
| `workspace:read-artifact` | `(runId, name)` → `{text, truncated?}` \| `{error}` |
| `workspace:abort-run` | `(runId)` → `{ok, detail?, error?}` |
| `workspace:fold` | `(runId)` → `{ok, already?, error?}` |
| `workspace:open-report` | `(runId)` → `{ok, error?}` |

**`RunRow`** = `{runId, runDir, status, startedAt, completedAt, bench: string[], chair, costDisplay, tierCounts|null, overallVerdict|null}` or error row `{runId, runDir|null, error, pointerPath, startedAt}`.

**`RunDetail`** = `{runId, runDir, run, tally, verdict, artifacts: {[name]: {present, bytes}}, derived}` where each of run/tally/verdict is the parsed doc, `{parseError, rawPath}`, or `null` (absent); top-level `{runId, error}` when the pointer/run.json is unusable. `derived` = `{schemaSupported, names: [{model,label}], stageRail: [{name,label,status,startedAt,completedAt}], matrix|null, cost: {rows:[{model,role,status,durationMs,costDisplay}], totalDisplay, costAmount|null, maxCost|null}, verdictPanel: {present, overallVerdict, tierCounts, streetCred, decisions, reason}}` (`derived: null` when run.json is unparseable).

**`MatrixModel`** = `{judges: [{model,label}], rows: [{id, severity, tier, thin, tierOverride, debate, raiser: {model,label}, basis: {a,d,n}, cells: [{judge:{model,label}, verdict|null, sym, isRaiser}]}], tierCounts, judged}` — `debate` (⚠️ DE-ROT F29) is `f.debate || null`, the v4.1 decoration `{action, previousTier}`; rows whose `action` is `withdrawn` must render de-emphasized (a `no-response` finding still stands).

> ⚠️ **DE-ROT (F07, F11) — two sources in this contract are wrong.**
> 1. **`tierOverride` and the post-override `tier` come from `verdict.json`, never `tally.json`.**
>    `tally()` unconditionally writes `tierOverride: null` on every finding
>    (`src/council/tally.js:106`; the shipped test says so outright at
>    `tests/council/tally.test.js:98-102`). Only `buildVerdict` materializes `{from,to,reason}` — and
>    it also rewrites `tier` to `tierOverride.to` (`src/council/verdict.js:33-37`). Reading tally for
>    either field is a silent no-op that passes its unit test against a fabricated fixture and
>    evaluates to `null` on every real run forever. **Widen the signature to
>    `buildMatrixModel(tally, labelMap, verdict)` and join `verdict.findings[]` by `id`** (Task 3
>    already loads verdict.json).
> 2. **`judges` has no stated source, and the shipped roster rule is name-gated, not vote-derived:**
>    `judges = tally.meta.claudeInCouncil ? tally.meta.models.filter(m => m !== 'claude') :
>    tally.meta.models` — mirroring `src/council/report.js:23-35`, whose comment explains that Claude
>    "is judged but never judges, so its reserved seat must never grow a matrix column," while a
>    bench judge with zero adjudications still needs its blank column. Deriving the roster from
>    observed votes breaks the second half; using `meta.models` raw breaks the first.

**`LiveModel`** = `{ok:true, view, runId, status, stageName, stages, seats: [Seat], costDisplay, costAmount, flags: {crashed, stalled, stalledForSeconds}, terminal}`; **`Seat`** = `{id, model, role, status, stage, messages, tokensIn, tokensOut, costDisplay, lastActivity, latestPreview, stalled}` (absent → `null` → rendered `—`).

> ⚠️ **DE-ROT (F01, F03, F34, F35, F39, F40) — reconciled with the shipped council payload.**
> This contract is achievable **only after Task 0.5**. Corrections:
> - **`seats`** maps from the `legs[]` Task 0.5 adds. Before Task 0.5 there is nothing to map.
> - **`flags`** — `crashed` exists nowhere in the codebase. Populate `flags.crashed` from
>   `status === 'error'` + `reason` (the council crash path), and `stalled`/`stalledForSeconds` from
>   the values Task 0.5 computes. Never derive them renderer-side (A4).
> - **`Seat.role`** is not on the live path — legs carry `model`, not council role. Either have
>   Task 0.5 stamp `role` from the stage that owns the leg, or render the Role column `—` while live
>   and populate it from `runStats` only once terminal. Say which; do not leave it implied.
> - **`Seat.lastActivity`** is not a relative time on main: `src/mcp-server.js:598` stamps
>   `leg.latestActivity` (an action label) and `:603` `leg.lastActivityAt` (a timestamp). Pick the
>   timestamp and format renderer-side, or rename the field.
> - **Add `legsTotal`/`legsComplete`** to `LiveModel` — they are the two counters the payload
>   already ships (`src/mcp-council-awareness.js:149`) and the current contract discards them. They
>   are the honest fallback readout if a seat row is ever unavailable.
> - **`costAmount`/`costDisplay`** come from `doc.usage`, which is the **active stage's** rollup, not
>   the run total (`src/mcp-council-awareness.js:137` scopes the loop to the active stage). Label the
>   live gauge as stage-scoped, or sum across stages in Task 0.5. Do not present it as a run total.
> - **`stages`** entries carry only `{name, status, waveId}` — **no timestamps**. The live refresh
>   must merge onto the durable `run.json` stage list rather than replacing it, or the start/end
>   times already on screen get wiped on the first tick.

**Renderer namespaces (script-tag globals, load order):** `window.AmicusMd` (md-lite) → `window.AmicusLive` (live-model) → `window.AmicusRender` → `window.AmicusMatrix` → app boot. The bridge is `window.amicusWorkspace.invoke(channel, ...args)`.

### Task 0.5: ⚠️ DE-ROT (F01) — emit `legs[]` + liveness flags on the composed council live doc

**Why this task exists.** The pre-flight found that the live Seats panel — the feature this whole rev
is pitched on — has no data source. `buildCouncilStatusPayload` computes per-leg enriched usage into
a local `usageLegs` array, sums it into `payload.usage`, and **throws the rows away**. The only
`legs[]` producer in the codebase is gated on `metadata.type === 'wave'`. Shipped v4.3 states this in
a test name: *"council doc: stage checklist rendered above (no legs on the composed council
payload)"*. Without this task, Tasks 14 and 15 both pass their unit tests (they feed a hand-written
doc containing a fabricated `legs` array) and render an empty panel against every real run.

This is the one place v4.4 touches the data layer. It is additive, it belongs to the module that owns
the document, and it lands **before** any GUI code consumes it.

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/mcp-council-awareness.js` (**191 lines today, hard-gated at 300** — if the addition
  pushes it over, extract the leg-row builder to `src/observe/council-legs.js` rather than
  grandfathering)
- Modify: `schemas/council-run-live.schema.json` (additive)
- Test: `tests/observe/council-live-legs.test.js` (new); extend `tests/schemas-live.test.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `waveLegIds`/`countWaveLegs` and `readProgress` (already used in this file), `isStalled`
  from `src/sidecar/progress.js`, `enrichLegUsage` from `src/observe/live-doc.js`.
- Produces: `payload.legs: [{taskId, model, role, status, messages, stage, latestPreview, lastActivityAt, stalled, usage?}]`
  plus `payload.stalled` / `payload.stalledForSeconds`. Consumed by Task 14 `live-normalize.js`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `tests/observe/council-live-legs.test.js` asserting, over a temp run dir with a running stage1
wave and three leg dirs:
1. `buildCouncilStatusPayload` returns `legs` with **one row per leg id**, including legs that have
   **not yet flushed usage** (this is the trap — see Step 2);
2. each row carries `taskId` + `model` + `status`, and `usage` only when priced;
3. a leg whose `progress.json` is older than the stall threshold sets `stalled: true` on that row and
   surfaces `payload.stalled`;
4. `legsTotal`/`legsComplete` are unchanged from today's values (no regression);
5. `view: 'live'` is still stamped for a non-terminal run.

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement — and do NOT reuse `usageLegs` for the rows**

> ⚠️ The naive one-line version (`payload.legs = usageLegs`) is **wrong**: the existing push at
> `src/mcp-council-awareness.js:144` is conditional on `enriched.usage`, so only legs that have
> already priced would appear — and just-started legs are exactly the ones a live seats panel most
> needs. Build the row array **separately** from the usage-rollup gate: push one row per leg id
> unconditionally, and let `usage` be an optional field on the row. Keep
> `if (usageLegs.length) { payload.usage = … }` untouched so the existing rollup behavior does not
> change.

Mirror the field names the wave branch already uses (`src/mcp-server.js:594-608`) so
`live-normalize.js` has one vocabulary to map, not two.

- [ ] **Step 3: Update the schema + verify no consumer regressed**

Add the new fields to `schemas/council-run-live.schema.json` (additive — `additionalProperties` is
already true, but the fields should be documented). Then confirm the existing council consumers still
behave: `src/observe/watch-render.js:44` loops `doc.legs || []`, so it will now start printing seat
rows for council runs in `amicus watch` — **that is a deliberate, welcome side effect**, but it
changes terminal output, so update `tests/observe/watch-render.test.js:38` (whose name asserts the
opposite) and mention it in the CHANGELOG at Task 19.

- [ ] **Step 4: Gates**

```bash
npx jest tests/observe tests/schemas-live.test.js
npm run lint && node scripts/check-file-sizes.js --all
```

Expected: green; `src/mcp-council-awareness.js` still under 300 lines.

- [ ] **Step 5: Commit**

```bash
git add src/mcp-council-awareness.js schemas/council-run-live.schema.json tests/observe
git commit -m "feat(observe): emit per-leg rows + stall flags on the composed council live doc" -m "Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5 <noreply@anthropic.com>"
```

## Phase 1 — Historical core (pure modules; zero Electron, zero v4.3)

Everything in this phase renders a council run from its run directory alone. It is deliberately first (spec §11): it de-risks the UI and depends only on v4.0.

### Task 1: Council run-dir fixtures

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/fixtures/council-run-complete/` (15 files, below)
- Create: `tests/fixtures/council-run-degraded/` (13 files — was "10 files"; see ⚠️ DE-ROT (F22) at Step 4)
- Create: `tests/fixtures/council-run-live/` (run.json + in-runDir sessions)
- Test: `tests/workspace/fixtures.test.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: v4.0 artifact shapes (spec-contract register).
- Produces: the three fixture dirs every later unit/e2e test reads. Bench is `["gemini", "gpt", "qwen"]`, chair `deepseek`, labelMap `{"Review A": "gemini", "Review B": "gpt", "Review C": "qwen"}`. RunIds: `aaaa1111` (complete), `bbbb2222` (degraded), `cccc3333` (live). Fixtures contain NO pointer files — tests seed pointer files into temp project dirs pointing at (copies of) these dirs.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing integrity test**

Create `tests/workspace/fixtures.test.js`:

```js
'use strict';

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

const FX = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'fixtures');
const COMPLETE = path.join(FX, 'council-run-complete');
const DEGRADED = path.join(FX, 'council-run-degraded');
const LIVE = path.join(FX, 'council-run-live');

const readJson = (dir, name) => JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(dir, name), 'utf-8'));

describe('council workspace fixtures', () => {
  test('complete run: envelope, bench, labelMap, all prose artifacts present', () => {
    const run = readJson(COMPLETE, 'run.json');
    expect(run.schemaVersion).toBe(2);
    expect(run.type).toBe('council-run');
    expect(run.runId).toBe('aaaa1111');
    expect(run.status).toBe('complete');
    expect(run.bench).toEqual(['gemini', 'gpt', 'qwen']);
    expect(run.labelMap['Review A']).toBe('gemini');
    for (const f of ['briefing-stage1.md', 'bundle-stage2.md', 'chair-packet.md', 'chair-output.md',
      'tally-input.json', 'tally.json', 'verdict.json', 'report.html',
      'review-gemini.md', 'review-gpt.md', 'review-qwen.md',
      'judge-gemini.md', 'judge-gpt.md', 'judge-qwen.md']) {
      expect(fs.existsSync(path.join(COMPLETE, f))).toBe(true);
    }
  });

  test('complete run: tally has the four tiers incl. one thin singleton', () => {
    const tally = readJson(COMPLETE, 'tally.json');
    expect(tally.tierCounts).toEqual({ Confirmed: 1, Contested: 1, Singleton: 1, Disputed: 1 });
    const c2 = tally.findings.find((f) => f.id === 'C2');
    expect(c2.confidence).toBe('thin');
  });

  test('complete run: chair prose contains a PLANTED fold marker (strip test bait)', () => {
    const chair = fs.readFileSync(path.join(COMPLETE, 'chair-output.md'), 'utf-8');
    expect(chair).toContain('[SIDECAR_FOLD:deadbeefdeadbeef]');
  });

  test('degraded run: partial, chair failed, overallVerdict null, chair-output.md absent', () => {
    const run = readJson(DEGRADED, 'run.json');
    expect(run.status).toBe('partial');
    expect(run.exitCode).toBe(2);
    const verdict = readJson(DEGRADED, 'verdict.json');
    expect(verdict.overallVerdict).toBeNull();
    expect(fs.existsSync(path.join(DEGRADED, 'chair-output.md'))).toBe(false);
  });

  test('live run: non-terminal with an active stage1 wave and leg progress files', () => {
    const run = readJson(LIVE, 'run.json');
    expect(run.status).toBe('running');
    expect(run.stages[0]).toMatchObject({ name: 'stage1', status: 'running', waveId: 'cccc3333-s1' });
    const legDir = path.join(LIVE, '.claude', 'amicus_sessions', 'dddd0001');
    expect(readJson(legDir, 'progress.json').type).toBe('progress');
  });

  // ⚠️ DE-ROT (F16): the engine writes ABSOLUTE paths into `stages[].project`
  // (run.js:127 `project: o.runDir`, run.js:166 `project: <runDir>/_scratch`,
  // run-chair.js:92) and into `options.outDir` (run.js:78). A relative "." would
  // make the live leg rollup resolve sessions against process.cwd(). Fixtures
  // therefore ship the `__RUNDIR__` sentinel; `copyRunFixture()` rewrites it.
  test('fixtures carry the __RUNDIR__ sentinel, never a relative path', () => {
    for (const dir of [COMPLETE, DEGRADED, LIVE]) {
      const run = readJson(dir, 'run.json');
      expect(run.options.outDir).toBe('__RUNDIR__');
      for (const s of run.stages) {
        if (s.project) { expect(s.project.startsWith('__RUNDIR__')).toBe(true); }
      }
    }
  });
});
```

> ⚠️ **DE-ROT (F16) — fixtures must not hard-code a relative `project`.** Add
> `tests/helpers/copy-run-fixture.js` (NOT under `tests/**/*.test.js`, so jest's
> `testMatch` ignores it) alongside this test:
>
> ```js
> // copyRunFixture(srcFixtureDir, destRunDir) → destRunDir
> function copyRunFixture(src, dest) {
>   fs.cpSync(src, dest, { recursive: true });
>   const p = path.join(dest, 'run.json');
>   const run = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(p, 'utf-8'));
>   if (run.options) { run.options.outDir = dest; }
>   for (const s of run.stages || []) {
>     if (typeof s.project === 'string' && s.project.startsWith('__RUNDIR__')) {
>       s.project = path.join(dest, s.project.slice('__RUNDIR__'.length)); // '' | '/_scratch'
>     }
>   }
>   fs.writeFileSync(p, JSON.stringify(run, null, 2));
>   return dest;
> }
> ```
>
> Every site that copies a fixture run dir must go through it: Task 15 Step 3's live
> smoke (`fs.cpSync(... 'council-run-live', runDir ...)`) and Task 18's
> `seedTempProject()` (`fs.cpSync(path.join(FX, 'council-run-live'), liveDir ...)`).
> Tasks 2–3 seed pointers straight at the read-only fixture dirs and never read
> `stages[].project`, so they are unaffected.


> ⚠️ **DE-ROT (F16) — `project` / `outDir` sentinels applied above.** Was `"."`,
> `"./_scratch"` and `"outDir": null`. The engine writes absolute paths
> (`run.js:78,127,166`, `run-chair.js:92`); `buildCouncilStatusPayload` uses
> `stage.project` as the PROJECT ROOT for `countWaveLegs`/`waveLegIds`
> (`mcp-council-awareness.js:137-146` → `getSessionDir`), so a relative value
> silently resolves against `process.cwd()` and the in-runDir legs vanish.
> Rewrite the sentinel on copy with `copyRunFixture()` (Step 1 note).

> ⚠️ **DE-ROT (F53) — `usage` is cost-only, and the cost object has six fields.**
> `finalize()` writes `usage: { cost: sumWaveUsage(allLegs).cost }` and DISCARDS
> the `tokens` half (`run.js:98-102`); `sumWaveUsage` returns
> `{amount, currency:'USD', source, reportedLegs, estimatedLegs, unpricedLegs}`
> (`pricing.js:104`). The `tokens` block above was removed and the six counters
> added. **Keep `amount: 0.4321` exactly** — rounding to `0.43` would make
> `formatCost` emit `$0.4300` (sub-$1 → `toFixed(4)`) and break the
> `'$0.4321'` assertions in Task 2 and Task 3.

> ⚠️ **DE-ROT (F57) — chair attempts live in `waveIds`, not `taskIds`.** Applied
> above. `run-chair.js:64,130` records every chair attempt with
> `appendStageWave(runDir,'chair',waveId)` → `stages[].waveIds`
> (`run-state.js:111-117`), and `subWaveIds` — the only sub-wave reader
> (`mcp-council-awareness.js:21-28`) — unions `waveId` + `waveIds`. `taskIds` is
> written on **stage1 only**, at completion, and holds LEG task ids
> (`run.js:132-135`), which is why stage1 now carries both. Same correction as
> the ⚠️ F13 row in the anchor table.

> ⚠️ **DE-ROT (F17) — streetCred recomputed from the run's own rankings, and
> `perJudgeRank` added.** `computeStreetCred` emits
> `{model, withSelf, peersOnly, perJudgeRank}` (`tally.js:49-65`) and `withSelf`
> differs from `peersOnly` ONLY when a judge ranks itself (`tally.js:58`
> `if (judge !== m)`). No fixture judge ranks itself, so the two are equal —
> `1.33`/`1.66` were unreachable. Values above are the verified output of
> `computeStreetCred` over the rankings shown (gemini→[gpt,qwen],
> gpt→[gemini,qwen], qwen→[gemini,gpt]). If ⚠️ F12's fix removes the `rankings`
> block from this file (rankings live in `tally-input.json`, not `tally.json`),
> keep those same orders there — these numbers are derived from them.
> `verdict.json` correctly omits `perJudgeRank` (`verdict.js:46` projects only
> `{model, withSelf, peersOnly}`).

> ⚠️ **DE-ROT (F18) — `conformance: "structured"` is not in the vocabulary.**
> The tri-state is exactly `clean | repaired | unstructured`
> (`run-assemble.js:27`, `run-stages.js:119,137,139`, `run-chair.js:125,141,145`,
> `tally.js:116` default). All four rows above were `"structured"`; they are now
> `clean`, with the **gpt** seat set to `repaired` so the middle state is covered
> by the corpus (a Stage-1 findings repair that succeeded — `run-stages.js:138`).
> Do the same in `verdict.json` below.

> always emits `{schemaVersion, type, stage, stageLabel, updatedAt, ...extra}`
> (`progress.js:113-124`; `stageLabel` is unconditional), and the extras the mirror
> writes are `messagesReceived` and `latestTool`
> (`conversation-mirror.js:99,116-123,164-169`). `readProgress` consumes exactly
> `progress.stageLabel` (:187), `progress.latestTool` (:193) and
> `progress.messagesReceived` (:198) — `messages`/`latest` are its OUTPUT field
> names, not its input. Keys corrected above; `usage.tokens` filled out to
> `emptyUsageTotals()`'s five counters and `costReported` set to `0` (a number —
> `pricing.js:14-34` sums it), never `null`.

> `messages > 0 && (latest === 'Working...' || latest === 'Executing tool call...')`,
> and `messages` is still 0 at that line for a leg dir holding only `progress.json`
> (it is topped up from `messagesReceived` afterwards, at :198). So that leg needs
> BOTH files — a `conversation.jsonl` whose last assistant entry is a name-less tool
> call (so `extractLatest` returns `'Executing tool call...'`, `progress.js:57-59`):
>
> ```
> {"role":"assistant","type":"tool_use","toolCall":{"id":"tc1","input":{}},"timestamp":"2026-07-19T10:00:58.000Z"}
> ```
>
> …plus `"latestTool": "bash", "stageLabel": "Calling tool: bash", "messagesReceived": 1`
> in its `progress.json` (the exact pair `conversation-mirror.js:116-123` writes),
> yielding `latest: 'Calling tool: bash'`. Give `dddd0003` a pre-output
> `{"stage":"prompt_sent","stageLabel":"Briefing delivered, waiting for response...","messagesReceived":0}`
> so the "no assistant output yet" branch is covered and all three seat rows still
> render. Workspace code must read these through
> `require('../sidecar/progress').readProgress(legDir)`, never `JSON.parse`.
>
> **Knock-on outside this task:** the two places that MUTATE this file mutate the
> dead key. Task 15 Step 3's smoke (`d.stage='complete';d.messages=9;d.latest='Complete';`)
> and Task 18's live e2e (`progress.messages = 9;`) must both set
> `messagesReceived = 9` (and `stageLabel = 'Complete'`) instead, or the seat row
> will never show `9` and both gates will hang to their deadline.

### Task 2: `src/workspace/run-scan.js` — pointer walk + run list

**Files:**
- Create: `src/workspace/run-scan.js`
- Test: `tests/workspace/run-scan.test.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: fixtures (Task 1); `formatCost` (`src/utils/pricing.js`); **`readPointer` (`src/council/run-state.js:134`) — v4.0, already merged, see F25**.
- Produces: `scanCouncilRuns(project) → RunRow[]` (sorted startedAt desc, error rows inline); `readPointer(project, runId) → {runId, runDir} | {runId, error}` (strips an optional `council-` prefix). Consumed by run-detail (Task 3), artifact-guard (Task 4), ipc-workspace (Task 8).

> ⚠️ **DE-ROT (F25) — this module's `readPointer` name-collides with a shipped one; wrap it, don't re-implement it.**
> `src/council/run-state.js:134 readPointer(project, runId)` already reads + validates the pointer and strips the
> `council-` prefix (`stripPrefix`, :119) — but it returns **`null`**, not `{runId, error}`. Step 3 below now
> delegates to it, so there is exactly one pointer parser in the tree and this wrapper is the single place that
> adapts `null → {runId, error}`. **Consequence for callers:** any consumer that imports the *shipped*
> `readPointer` directly (Task 8's `workspace:open-report`) must guard with `if (!ptr || ptr.error)` — a bare
> `ptr.error` throws a TypeError on null.
> For the walk itself, the shipped parallel is `listCouncilRuns` (`src/mcp-council-awareness.js:160`, over
> `run-state.listPointers` :142). `scanCouncilRuns` deliberately diverges — it emits error rows where
> `listCouncilRuns` silently `continue`s — so keep the local `readdirSync` walk, but match the shipped pointer
> pattern (`/^council-[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{1,64}\.json$/`, run-state.js:148) rather than hand-narrowing it to 8 hex.

> ⚠️ **DE-ROT (F24) — `costDisplay` is verbatim `formatCost` output; never hand-round it.**
> `formatCost` (`src/utils/pricing.js:114-120`) renders `amount < 1` with **4** decimals, `>= 1` with 2, prefixes
> `~` when `source` is `estimated`/`mixed`, and returns `—` (or `?` when `source === 'unknown'`) for a null amount.
> So `'~$0.31'` is **unreachable** — every sub-dollar total renders `$0.xxxx`. The Step 1 assertion is corrected
> below; `'$0.4321'` (amount 0.4321, source `reported`) is already right and stays. The identical trap exists in
> **Task 14** (`expect(m.costDisplay).toBe('~$0.06')` → must be `'~$0.0600'`) — fix it there too.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `tests/workspace/run-scan.test.js`:

```js
'use strict';

const fs = require('fs');
const os = require('os');
const path = require('path');

const { scanCouncilRuns, readPointer } = require('../../src/workspace/run-scan');

const FX = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'fixtures');

/** Seed a temp project whose sessions dir points at the given fixture dirs. */
function seedProject(entries) {
  const project = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'ws-scan-'));
  const sessions = path.join(project, '.claude', 'amicus_sessions');
  fs.mkdirSync(sessions, { recursive: true });
  for (const [runId, runDir] of Object.entries(entries)) {
    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(sessions, `council-${runId}.json`), JSON.stringify({ runId, runDir }));
  }
  return project;
}

describe('scanCouncilRuns', () => {
  test('builds rows for every pointer, sorted startedAt desc', () => {
    const project = seedProject({
      aaaa1111: path.join(FX, 'council-run-complete'),
      bbbb2222: path.join(FX, 'council-run-degraded'),
      cccc3333: path.join(FX, 'council-run-live'),
    });
    const rows = scanCouncilRuns(project);
    expect(rows.map((r) => r.runId)).toEqual(['cccc3333', 'aaaa1111', 'bbbb2222']);
    const complete = rows.find((r) => r.runId === 'aaaa1111');
    expect(complete.status).toBe('complete');
    expect(complete.bench).toEqual(['gemini', 'gpt', 'qwen']);
    expect(complete.chair).toBe('deepseek');
    expect(complete.overallVerdict).toBe('Fix these first');
    expect(complete.costDisplay).toBe('$0.4321');
    const degraded = rows.find((r) => r.runId === 'bbbb2222');
    expect(degraded.overallVerdict).toBeNull();
    // ⚠️ DE-ROT (F24): was '~$0.31' — unreachable. formatCost gives any amount < 1
    // four decimals (pricing.js:118), so {amount:0.31, source:'estimated'} → '~$0.3100'.
    expect(degraded.costDisplay).toBe('~$0.3100');
  });

  test('dangling pointer and unreadable run.json become error rows, never throw', () => {
    const project = seedProject({ eeee4444: path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'ws-scan-nowhere-' + Date.now()) });
    const badDir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'ws-scan-bad-'));
    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(badDir, 'run.json'), '{not json');
    fs.writeFileSync(
      path.join(project, '.claude', 'amicus_sessions', 'council-ffff5555.json'),
      JSON.stringify({ runId: 'ffff5555', runDir: badDir })
    );
    const rows = scanCouncilRuns(project);
    expect(rows).toHaveLength(2);
    for (const row of rows) { expect(row.error).toBeTruthy(); }
  });

  test('non-council files in the sessions dir are ignored; missing dir yields []', () => {
    const project = seedProject({ aaaa1111: path.join(FX, 'council-run-complete') });
    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(project, '.claude', 'amicus_sessions', 'notes.txt'), 'x');
    fs.mkdirSync(path.join(project, '.claude', 'amicus_sessions', 'dddd0001'));
    expect(scanCouncilRuns(project)).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(scanCouncilRuns(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'ws-scan-empty-' + Date.now()))).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe('readPointer', () => {
  test('resolves runDir, accepts the council- prefix, errors on missing pointer', () => {
    const project = seedProject({ aaaa1111: path.join(FX, 'council-run-complete') });
    expect(readPointer(project, 'aaaa1111').runDir).toBe(path.join(FX, 'council-run-complete'));
    expect(readPointer(project, 'council-aaaa1111').runDir).toBe(path.join(FX, 'council-run-complete'));
    expect(readPointer(project, '99999999').error).toBeTruthy();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `src/workspace/run-scan.js`**

```js
/**
 * Council Workspace — run discovery (v4.4 spec §4.3 / §5.1).
 *
 * Walks the project sessions dir for v4.0 council pointer files
 * (`council-<runId>.json` = {runId, runDir}) and builds run-list rows from a
 * shallow, defensive read of run.json (+ verdict.json for the overallVerdict
 * chip). Strictly read-only (§6.2). Unreadable pointers/runs come back as
 * {runId, error} rows — this module never throws on bad input.
 */
'use strict';

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const { formatCost } = require('../utils/pricing');
// ⚠️ DE-ROT (F25): v4.0 module (not v4.3) — safe for Phase 1. Single source of truth
// for reading/validating council pointer files; do not re-parse them here.
const runState = require('../council/run-state');

// ⚠️ DE-ROT (F25): widened from /^council-([0-9a-f]{8})\.json$/ to match the shipped
// walker's pattern (src/council/run-state.js:148) so ids that are not 8 hex still list.
const POINTER_RE = /^council-([a-zA-Z0-9_-]{1,64})\.json$/;

function sessionsDir(project) {
  return path.join(project, '.claude', 'amicus_sessions');
}

function readJson(file) {
  try { return { doc: JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf-8')) }; }
  catch (err) { return { error: err.message }; }
}

/** Best-effort sort timestamp: run.json createdAt || first stage start || pointer mtime. */
function startedAtOf(run, pointerPath) {
  if (run) {
    if (run.createdAt) { return run.createdAt; }
    const s0 = Array.isArray(run.stages) && run.stages[0];
    if (s0 && s0.startedAt) { return s0.startedAt; }
  }
  try { return fs.statSync(pointerPath).mtime.toISOString(); } catch { return null; }
}

/**
 * Resolve a council runId to its run dir via the pointer file.
 * Accepts the id with or without the `council-` prefix (v4.0 readPointer parity).
 * ⚠️ DE-ROT (F25): thin ADAPTER over the shipped src/council/run-state.js:134
 * readPointer — that one already strips the prefix (:119) and validates
 * {runId, runDir}, but returns null. This wrapper is the ONLY place that turns
 * null into the {runId, error} row the workspace run list renders (§5.1).
 * @returns {{runId: string, runDir: string} | {runId: string, error: string}}
 */
function readPointer(project, runId) {
  const id = String(runId).replace(/^council-/, '');
  const ptr = runState.readPointer(project, id);
  if (!ptr) { return { runId: id, error: 'pointer missing, unreadable, or invalid' }; }
  return { runId: id, runDir: ptr.runDir };
}

/**
 * @param {string} project absolute project dir (AMICUS_PROJECT)
 * @returns {Array<object>} RunRow[] sorted startedAt desc; error rows inline.
 */
function scanCouncilRuns(project) {
  let names = [];
  try { names = fs.readdirSync(sessionsDir(project)); } catch { return []; }
  const rows = [];
  for (const name of names) {
    const m = POINTER_RE.exec(name);
    if (!m) { continue; }
    const pointerPath = path.join(sessionsDir(project), name);
    const ptr = readPointer(project, m[1]);
    if (ptr.error) {
      rows.push({ runId: m[1], runDir: null, error: ptr.error, pointerPath, startedAt: startedAtOf(null, pointerPath) });
      continue;
    }
    const r = readJson(path.join(ptr.runDir, 'run.json'));
    if (r.error) {
      rows.push({ runId: m[1], runDir: ptr.runDir, error: `run.json: ${r.error}`, pointerPath, startedAt: startedAtOf(null, pointerPath) });
      continue;
    }
    const run = r.doc;
    const row = {
      runId: run.runId || m[1],
      runDir: ptr.runDir,
      status: run.status || 'unknown',
      startedAt: startedAtOf(run, pointerPath),
      completedAt: run.completedAt || null,
      bench: Array.isArray(run.bench) ? run.bench : [],
      chair: run.chair || null,
      costDisplay: formatCost(run.usage && run.usage.cost),
      tierCounts: null,
      overallVerdict: null,
    };
    const v = readJson(path.join(ptr.runDir, 'verdict.json'));
    if (!v.error && v.doc) {
      row.overallVerdict = v.doc.overallVerdict === undefined ? null : v.doc.overallVerdict;
      row.tierCounts = v.doc.tierCounts || null;
    }
    rows.push(row);
  }
  rows.sort((a, b) => String(b.startedAt || '').localeCompare(String(a.startedAt || '')));
  return rows;
}

module.exports = { scanCouncilRuns, readPointer, POINTER_RE };
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `src/workspace/run-detail.js`**

```js
/**
 * Council Workspace — run detail: defensive parse of run.json / tally.json /
 * verdict.json + the derived view models the renderer paints (v4.4 §4.5
 * workspace:get-run, §5.2, §9). Malformed JSON yields {parseError, rawPath}
 * per document — one bad file never blanks the whole run (spec §9 row 1).
 * Read-only (§6.2).
 */
'use strict';

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const { formatCost } = require('../utils/pricing');
const { readPointer } = require('./run-scan');
const { buildNamePairs } = require('./blind-mode');
const { buildMatrixModel } = require('./matrix-model');
const { artifactAllowlist } = require('./artifact-guard');

/**
 * Shared terminal-status list (also mirrored renderer-side in live-model.js).
 * ⚠️ DE-ROT (F26): deliberate HAND-COPY of src/observe/live-doc.js:18 `TERMINAL`,
 * names and order byte-identical. Do NOT require() it — Task 3 is Phase 1
 * ("zero v4.3") and live-doc.js is v4.3. Not to be confused with the shipped
 * src/utils/result-schema.js:13 TERMINAL_STATUSES, which is the LEG set (no 'partial').
 */
const TERMINAL_STATUSES = ['complete', 'partial', 'error', 'crashed', 'aborted', 'timeout', 'idle-timeout'];

/** Friendly labels for known v4.0 stage names; unknown names (e.g. v4.1
 *  debate) pass through raw — the graceful-when-present rule (spec §5.2). */
const STAGE_LABELS = {
  stage1: 'Stage 1 · reviews',
  repairs: 'Repairs',
  debate: 'Debate',
  stage2: 'Stage 2 · judging',
  tally: 'Tally',
  chair: 'Chair',
  verdict: 'Verdict',
};

function readDoc(runDir, name) {
  const rawPath = path.join(runDir, name);
  if (!fs.existsSync(rawPath)) { return null; }
  try { return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(rawPath, 'utf-8')); }
  catch (err) { return { parseError: err.message, rawPath }; }
}

function stageRail(run) {
  const stages = Array.isArray(run.stages) ? run.stages : [];
  return stages.map((s) => ({
    name: s.name,
    label: STAGE_LABELS[s.name] || String(s.name),
    status: s.status || 'pending',
    startedAt: s.startedAt || null,
    completedAt: s.completedAt || null,
  }));
}

function costPanel(run, tally) {
  const stats = tally && Array.isArray(tally.runStats) ? tally.runStats : [];
  const rows = stats.map((r) => ({
    model: r.model,
    role: r.role || (r.wasChair ? 'chair' : 'seat'),
    status: r.status || 'unknown',
    durationMs: r.durationMs === undefined ? null : r.durationMs,
    costDisplay: formatCost(r.usage && r.usage.cost),
  }));
  const cost = run.usage && run.usage.cost ? run.usage.cost : null;
  return {
    rows,
    totalDisplay: formatCost(cost),
    costAmount: cost && typeof cost.amount === 'number' ? cost.amount : null,
    maxCost: run.options && typeof run.options.maxCost === 'number' ? run.options.maxCost : null,
  };
}

function verdictPanel(run, verdict) {
  // ⚠️ DE-ROT (F04): `run.error` is an OBJECT `{code, message}`, never a string — and it is
  // NULL on exactly the runs this panel exists to explain. See the note below.
  const reason = run.error ? `${run.error.code}: ${run.error.message}` : null;
  if (!verdict || verdict.parseError) {
    return { present: false, overallVerdict: null, tierCounts: null, streetCred: [], decisions: [], reason };
  }
  return {
    present: true,
    overallVerdict: verdict.overallVerdict === undefined ? null : verdict.overallVerdict,
    tierCounts: verdict.tierCounts || null,
    streetCred: Array.isArray(verdict.streetCred) ? verdict.streetCred : [],
    decisions: (Array.isArray(verdict.findings) ? verdict.findings : [])
      .filter((f) => f.decision)
      .map((f) => ({ id: f.id, decision: f.decision, applied: f.applied === true })),
    reason,
  };
}
```

> ⚠️ **DE-ROT (F04) — `reason` is dead on exactly the runs it was designed for.**
> Two separate defects:
> 1. **Shape.** `run.error` is `{code, message}` — every producer passes an object
>    (`COUNCIL_QUORUM`, `COST_EXCEEDED`, `INTERNAL`, `COUNCIL_CLAUDE_REVIEW_INVALID`), and the crash
>    detector writes `{code:'INTERNAL', message:'Council engine process exited unexpectedly'}`. The
>    original `run.error || null` would put an object where a string is expected; downstream,
>    `renderBanner` assigns it via `textContent`, so a real exit-1 run renders the literal
>    `[object Object]`. Fixed inline above — and **check the Task 13 banner call site too**, which
>    passes `d.run.error` raw.
> 2. **Availability.** The degraded path this panel exists to explain — chair failed, `status:
>    'partial'`, exit 2 — calls `finalize(degraded.value ? 2 : 0)` with **no error argument**
>    (`src/council/run.js:293`), so `run.error` is `null` there. `error` is non-null only when
>    `exitCode === 1`. So "why is `overallVerdict` null?" cannot be answered from `run.error` on the
>    common degraded run. **Derive the degraded explanation from the stage list instead** — find the
>    stage whose `status` is `'error'`/`'timeout'` and name it ("chair stage failed") — and keep
>    `run.error` as the exit-1 path only. The Task 3 test asserting a string here passes today only
>    because the fixture invents an `error` the engine would not have written (see F21).

```js
/**
 * @param {string} project
 * @param {string} runId (with or without the council- prefix)
 * @returns {object} RunDetail (see plan Shared contracts)
 */
function getRunDetail(project, runId) {
  const ptr = readPointer(project, runId);
  if (ptr.error) { return { runId: ptr.runId, error: ptr.error }; }
  const runDir = ptr.runDir;
  const run = readDoc(runDir, 'run.json');
  if (!run) { return { runId: ptr.runId, runDir, error: 'run.json missing' }; }
  const tally = readDoc(runDir, 'tally.json');
  const verdict = readDoc(runDir, 'verdict.json');

  let derived = null;
  if (!run.parseError) {
    const labelMap = run.labelMap && typeof run.labelMap === 'object' ? run.labelMap : {};
    const tallyOk = tally && !tally.parseError ? tally : null;
    derived = {
      schemaSupported: run.schemaVersion === 2,
      names: buildNamePairs(labelMap),
      stageRail: stageRail(run),
      // ⚠️ DE-ROT (F07): 3-arg signature — tierOverride + post-override tier live in verdict.json.
      matrix: tallyOk ? buildMatrixModel(tallyOk, labelMap, verdict && !verdict.parseError ? verdict : null) : null,
      cost: costPanel(run, tallyOk),
      verdictPanel: verdictPanel(run, verdict),
    };
  }

  const artifacts = {};
  const names = run.parseError ? [] : artifactAllowlist(run);
  for (const name of [...names, 'report.html', 'run.json', 'tally.json', 'verdict.json']) {
    try {
      const st = fs.statSync(path.join(runDir, name));
      artifacts[name] = { present: true, bytes: st.size };
    } catch {
      artifacts[name] = { present: false, bytes: 0 };
    }
  }

  return { runId: ptr.runId, runDir, run, tally, verdict, artifacts, derived };
}

module.exports = { getRunDetail, TERMINAL_STATUSES, STAGE_LABELS };
```

```js
'use strict';

const fs = require('fs');
const os = require('os');
const path = require('path');

const { artifactAllowlist, readRunArtifact, MAX_ARTIFACT_BYTES } = require('../../src/workspace/artifact-guard');

const FX = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'fixtures');

function seedProject(runDir) {
  const project = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'ws-guard-'));
  const sessions = path.join(project, '.claude', 'amicus_sessions');
  fs.mkdirSync(sessions, { recursive: true });
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(sessions, 'council-aaaa1111.json'), JSON.stringify({ runId: 'aaaa1111', runDir }));
  return project;
}

describe('artifactAllowlist', () => {
  test('fixed names plus sanitized review-/judge- files per bench seat', () => {
    const run = { bench: ['gemini', 'openrouter/deepseek/deepseek-chat'] };
    const list = artifactAllowlist(run);
    expect(list).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining([
      'briefing-stage1.md', 'bundle-stage2.md', 'chair-packet.md', 'chair-output.md', 'tally-input.json',
      'review-gemini.md', 'judge-gemini.md',
      'review-openrouter-deepseek-deepseek-chat.md', 'judge-openrouter-deepseek-deepseek-chat.md',
    ]));
    expect(list.some((n) => n.includes('/') || n.includes('\\') || n.includes('..'))).toBe(false);
  });

  test('missing/invalid bench yields fixed names only', () => {
    expect(artifactAllowlist({})).toHaveLength(5);
    expect(artifactAllowlist({ bench: 'nope' })).toHaveLength(5);
  });

  // ⚠️ DE-ROT (F28): v4.1 `--debate` runs write five MORE artifact kinds. They are gated
  // on run.debate, so both `toHaveLength(5)` asserts above stay correct as written.
  test('a --debate run also allows the v4.1 debate artifacts', () => {
    const list = artifactAllowlist({ bench: ['gemini'], debate: { enabled: true, outcome: 'ran' } });
    expect(list).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining([
      'tally-provisional.json', 'revote-bundle.md', 'debate.json', 'rebuttal-gemini.md', 'revote-gemini.md',
    ]));
    expect(artifactAllowlist({ bench: ['gemini'] })).not.toContain('debate.json');
  });
});

describe('readRunArtifact', () => {
  test('reads an allowlisted artifact from the fixture run', () => {
    const project = seedProject(path.join(FX, 'council-run-complete'));
    const res = readRunArtifact(project, 'aaaa1111', 'chair-output.md');
    expect(res.text).toContain('VERDICT: Fix these first');
    expect(res.truncated).toBeUndefined();
  });

  test('rejects names not on the allowlist (traversal cannot even be expressed)', () => {
    const project = seedProject(path.join(FX, 'council-run-complete'));
    for (const bad of ['../secrets.txt', '..\\secrets.txt', 'run.json', 'review-notabench.md', 'report.html']) {
      expect(readRunArtifact(project, 'aaaa1111', bad).error).toBeTruthy();
    }
  });

  test('not-yet-written artifact reports a friendly error', () => {
    const project = seedProject(path.join(FX, 'council-run-degraded'));
    const res = readRunArtifact(project, 'bbbb2222', 'chair-output.md');
    expect(res.error).toMatch(/not written yet/);
  });

  test('realpath escape (symlinked artifact) is refused — injected realpath', () => {
    const project = seedProject(path.join(FX, 'council-run-complete'));
    const outside = path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'outside.md');
    const res = readRunArtifact(project, 'aaaa1111', 'chair-output.md', {
      realpathSync: (p) => (p.endsWith('chair-output.md') ? outside : p),
    });
    expect(res.error).toMatch(/escapes run directory/);
  });

  test('oversized artifact is truncated with a flag', () => {
    const runDir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'ws-guard-big-'));
    fs.copyFileSync(path.join(FX, 'council-run-complete', 'run.json'), path.join(runDir, 'run.json'));
    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(runDir, 'bundle-stage2.md'), 'x'.repeat(MAX_ARTIFACT_BYTES + 5000));
    const project = seedProject(runDir);
    const res = readRunArtifact(project, 'aaaa1111', 'bundle-stage2.md');
    expect(res.truncated).toBe(true);
    expect(Buffer.byteLength(res.text, 'utf8')).toBeLessThanOrEqual(MAX_ARTIFACT_BYTES);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `src/workspace/artifact-guard.js`**

```js
/**
 * Council Workspace — artifact read guard (v4.4 §4.5 workspace:read-artifact).
 *
 * Two independent fences:
 *  1. The name must be on the manifest-derived allowlist (fixed names +
 *     review-/judge- files for run.json bench seats via v4.0's sanitizeName,
 *     which strips every path separator) — traversal is unrepresentable.
 *  2. The realpath of the resolved file must stay inside the realpath of the
 *     run dir — a symlinked artifact cannot leak files from outside.
 * >200 KB truncates with a flag (spec §4.5). report.html is deliberately NOT
 * readable here — it opens externally via workspace:open-report.
 */
'use strict';

const fsReal = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const { sanitizeName } = require('../council/run-launch');
const { readPointer } = require('./run-scan');

const FIXED_ARTIFACTS = ['briefing-stage1.md', 'bundle-stage2.md', 'chair-packet.md', 'chair-output.md', 'tally-input.json'];
// ⚠️ DE-ROT (F28): v4.1's debate stage writes five MORE run-dir artifact kinds the original
// allowlist never named, so the Workspace hard-refused every `--debate` output with
// `artifact not allowed: <name>`. Writers: tally-provisional.json = src/council/run.js:199;
// revote-bundle.md = run-debate.js:119; debate.json = run-debate.js:261; the per-seat
// rebuttal-/revote- pair = materializeDebate (run-launch.js:127-136).
const DEBATE_ARTIFACTS = ['tally-provisional.json', 'revote-bundle.md', 'debate.json'];
const MAX_ARTIFACT_BYTES = 200 * 1024;

/** @param {object} run parsed run.json (may be partial) */
function artifactAllowlist(run) {
  const names = [...FIXED_ARTIFACTS];
  const bench = run && Array.isArray(run.bench) ? run.bench : [];
  // ⚠️ DE-ROT (F28): run.json carries a `debate` key ONLY on --debate runs, and it is seeded
  // on the FIRST write (src/council/run.js:74-77), so this gate is safe and keeps the
  // allowlist tight for the common case.
  const debated = !!(run && run.debate);
  if (debated) { names.push(...DEBATE_ARTIFACTS); }
  for (const m of bench) {
    names.push(`review-${sanitizeName(m)}.md`);
    names.push(`judge-${sanitizeName(m)}.md`);
    // rebuttal-/revote- are keyed on the same BENCH ALIAS through the same sanitizeName
    // (materializeDebate is called with `d.raiser` / the revote leg's model — both aliases).
    if (debated) {
      names.push(`rebuttal-${sanitizeName(m)}.md`);
      names.push(`revote-${sanitizeName(m)}.md`);
    }
  }
  return names;
}

/**
 * @param {string} project
 * @param {string} runId
 * @param {string} name artifact filename (must be allowlisted)
 * @param {object} [deps] test injection: {realpathSync}
 * @returns {{text: string, truncated?: true} | {error: string}}
 */
function readRunArtifact(project, runId, name, deps = {}) {
  const realpathSync = deps.realpathSync || ((p) => fsReal.realpathSync(p));
  const ptr = readPointer(project, runId);
  if (ptr.error) { return { error: ptr.error }; }
  let run;
  try { run = JSON.parse(fsReal.readFileSync(path.join(ptr.runDir, 'run.json'), 'utf-8')); }
  catch (err) { return { error: `run.json: ${err.message}` }; }

  if (!artifactAllowlist(run).includes(name)) { return { error: `artifact not allowed: ${name}` }; }

  let realDir;
  try { realDir = realpathSync(ptr.runDir); }
  catch (err) { return { error: `run dir unreadable: ${err.message}` }; }
  let realTarget;
  try { realTarget = realpathSync(path.join(ptr.runDir, name)); }
  catch { return { error: `not written yet: ${name}` }; }
  if (realTarget !== realDir && !String(realTarget).startsWith(realDir + path.sep)) {
    return { error: 'artifact escapes run directory' };
  }

  let buf;
  try { buf = fsReal.readFileSync(realTarget); }
  catch (err) { return { error: err.message }; }
  if (buf.length > MAX_ARTIFACT_BYTES) {
    return { text: buf.subarray(0, MAX_ARTIFACT_BYTES).toString('utf-8'), truncated: true };
  }
  return { text: buf.toString('utf-8') };
}

module.exports = { artifactAllowlist, readRunArtifact, FIXED_ARTIFACTS, DEBATE_ARTIFACTS, MAX_ARTIFACT_BYTES };
```

> ⚠️ **DE-ROT (F28) — the allowlist fix is necessary but not sufficient; two follow-throughs.**
> Extending the allowlist only makes the debate names *readable*.
> - **Task 3 inherits it for free** — its `artifacts` presence map is built from
>   `artifactAllowlist(run)`, so the debate names appear there with no extra edit.
> - **Task 13 does NOT.** Its `proseLoader` panels request a hard-coded `files()` list and
>   never ask for `debate.json` / `rebuttal-*.md` / `revote-*.md`, so a `--debate` run would
>   still surface nothing. Add a debate panel (or extend the peer-packet panel) in Task 13.
> - The design doc's stale 7-name artifact list
>   (`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-19-v4.4-council-workspace-design.md:216`) must be
>   updated in the Task 19 docs sweep.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/workspace/blind-mode.js`
- Create: `src/workspace/matrix-model.js`
- Test: `tests/workspace/blind-mode.test.js`, `tests/workspace/matrix-model.test.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `SYMBOL` from `src/council/report.js` (single source — ambiguity resolution 1); labelMap direction `{ 'Review A': model }` (v4.0 `assignLabels`); **⚠️ DE-ROT (F07): `verdict.json` as well as `tally.json`.**
- Produces: `buildNamePairs(labelMap) → [{label, model}]` (label-sorted), `labelFor(model, labelMap)`, `pairFor(model, labelMap) → {model, label}`; **`buildMatrixModel(tally, labelMap, verdict) → MatrixModel`** (⚠️ DE-ROT F07 — signature widened; see below).

> ⚠️ **DE-ROT (F07) — the matrix reads `tierOverride` from the one file that never contains it.**
> `tally()` writes `tierOverride: null` on **every** finding, unconditionally
> (`src/council/tally.js:106`), and tally.json is persisted verbatim from that record. The shipped
> unit test states the rule outright: *"tally never records overrides (that's buildVerdict's job)"*
> (`tests/council/tally.test.js:98-102`). Only `buildVerdict` materializes `{from,to,reason}` — and
> it **also rewrites `tier` to `tierOverride.to`** (`src/council/verdict.js:33-37`).
>
> So both the badge **and the tier the row renders** must come from `verdict.json`. As written this
> is a textbook silent no-op: `tierOverride: f.tierOverride || null` passes its unit test against the
> fabricated Task 1 fixture and then evaluates to `null` on every real run forever, so spec §5.2's
> "tierOverride shown when set" badge never renders — and the row shows the *pre*-override tier, so
> even a correct badge would contradict its own row.
>
> **Fix:** widen to `buildMatrixModel(tally, labelMap, verdict)` and join `verdict.findings[]` by
> `id` (Task 3 already loads verdict.json — plan Shared contracts). Where verdict.json is absent or
> unparseable, fall back to tally's tier and render no badge. Also fix the **Task 1 fixture** so
> tally.json carries `tierOverride: null` like the engine emits, and move the C1 override assertion
> onto the verdict-sourced path.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `src/workspace/blind-mode.js`**

```js
/**
 * Council Workspace — blind-mode name mapping (v4.4 §6.3).
 *
 * Pure inversion helpers over run.json labelMap ({ 'Review A': model }).
 * Finding ids are ALREADY label-space (A1, B2 — v4.0 anonymize), so blind
 * mode never touches ids; it only swaps model display names for labels.
 * Bias hygiene, not security (spec §6.1) — the map is user-readable on disk.
 */
'use strict';

function buildNamePairs(labelMap) {
  if (!labelMap || typeof labelMap !== 'object') { return []; }
  const pairs = Object.entries(labelMap)
    .filter(([label, model]) => typeof label === 'string' && typeof model === 'string')
    .map(([label, model]) => ({ label, model }));
  pairs.sort((a, b) => a.label.localeCompare(b.label));
  return pairs;
}

function labelFor(model, labelMap) {
  if (!labelMap || typeof labelMap !== 'object') { return null; }
  for (const [label, m] of Object.entries(labelMap)) {
    if (m === model) { return label; }
  }
  return null;
}

function pairFor(model, labelMap) {
  return { model, label: labelFor(model, labelMap) };
}

module.exports = { buildNamePairs, labelFor, pairFor };
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `src/workspace/matrix-model.js`**

```js
/**
 * Council Workspace — adjudication matrix view model (v4.4 §5.2).
 *
 * Pure: tally.json + labelMap → renderable rows/cells. Symbols come from
 * council/report.js SYMBOL (single source — the report and the workspace can
 * never disagree about what ✓/✗/– mean). Every name-bearing field carries
 * BOTH spellings ({model, label}) so the renderer's blind toggle is a pure
 * display flip with no re-fetch. Missing votes (partial waves) are blank
 * cells, never invented neutrals — tier math already excluded them (v4.0).
 */
'use strict';

const { SYMBOL } = require('../council/report');
const { pairFor } = require('./blind-mode');

/**
 * @param {object} tally parsed tally.json
 * @param {object} labelMap run.json labelMap
 * @returns {object} MatrixModel (see plan Shared contracts)
 */
function buildMatrixModel(tally, labelMap) {
  const map = labelMap || {};
  const judges = tally && tally.meta && Array.isArray(tally.meta.models) ? tally.meta.models : [];
  const findings = tally && Array.isArray(tally.findings) ? tally.findings : [];
  const rows = findings.map((f) => {
    const votes = {};
    for (const adj of (Array.isArray(f.adjudications) ? f.adjudications : [])) {
      votes[adj.judge] = adj.verdict;
    }
    return {
      id: f.id,
      severity: f.severity || null,
      tier: f.tier || null,
      thin: f.confidence === 'thin',
      tierOverride: f.tierOverride || null,
      // ⚠️ DE-ROT (F29): v4.1 decorates tally.json findings in place with
      // `debate: {action, previousTier}` (src/council/debate.js:71-75; action ∈
      // defended|amended|withdrawn|no-response) and verdict.json carries it through
      // (src/council/verdict.js:43). Dropping it renders a WITHDRAWN finding as an
      // ordinary live row. Absent on non-debate runs, hence `|| null`.
      debate: f.debate || null,
      raiser: pairFor(f.raiser, map),
      basis: f.basis || { a: 0, d: 0, n: 0 },
      cells: judges.map((j) => {
        const verdict = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(votes, j) ? votes[j] : null;
        return {
          judge: pairFor(j, map),
          verdict,
          sym: verdict ? (SYMBOL[verdict] || '?') : ' ',
          isRaiser: j === f.raiser,
        };
      }),
    };
  });
  return {
    judges: judges.map((j) => pairFor(j, map)),
    rows,
    tierCounts: (tally && tally.tierCounts) || null,
    judged: !(tally && tally.judged === false),
  };
}

module.exports = { buildMatrixModel };
```

> ⚠️ **DE-ROT (F29) — how Tasks 13/15 must read `row.debate`. `no-response` is NOT a retraction.**
> - `withdrawn` — the raiser retracted it. Badge/de-emphasize as *"withdrawn — no longer live"*
>   (mirrors `src/council/report.js:132-139`). This is the only action that removes a row from
>   the live narrative.
> - `no-response` — the defense leg died or stayed unstructured, so **the original stands**
>   (`src/council/run-debate.js:26,198`). It is a fully live row: annotate it
>   *"(no response — original stands)"* (`report.js:145-151`); never grey it out.
> - `defended` / `amended` — render a `previousTier → tier` movement arrow, and only when the
>   two differ (`report.js:61-63, 140-144`).
>
> Also add `debate` to the **`MatrixModel` row shape** in the plan's Shared contracts section
> (owned by another region — flag it rather than editing here).

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `src/workspace/fold-format.js`**

```js
/**
 * Council Workspace — fold payload builder (v4.4 §7).
 *
 * MIRRORS the shipped fold-header builder `formatFoldOutput`
 * (src/headless.js:775-789, exported at :797, re-exported from src/index.js:20)
 * — byte-for-byte the same 8-line head. src/headless.js is the SOURCE OF
 * TRUTH; keep the head in sync with it. The duplication is deliberate:
 * requiring headless.js transitively pulls opencode-client / progress /
 * conversation-mirror at require time (src/headless.js:8-17), which
 * src/workspace/ must stay free of.
 * ⚠️ DE-ROT (F58): the header used to claim it "reuses the v4.0 marker/nonce
 * contract exactly" while silently dropping formatFoldOutput's nonce-required
 * throw (src/headless.js:776-778). The guard is restored in buildFoldText below.
 * No model call — the chair result already exists on disk, so a workspace fold
 * is a local read+format.
 * The chair body is UNTRUSTED model text: it passes through stripFoldMarkers
 * before embedding, so chair prose containing a marker can never truncate or
 * spoof the fold (the exact hazard the nonce closure exists for).
 * Degradation mirrors the engine's ladder: no chair → VERDICT: none + tally
 * summary; pre-tally → stage/status summary. Never blocked, always labeled.
 */
'use strict';

const { buildFoldMarker, stripFoldMarkers } = require('../utils/fold-marker');
const { formatCost } = require('../utils/pricing');

function ok(doc) { return doc && !doc.parseError ? doc : null; }

function tierLine(tierCounts) {
  const t = tierCounts || {};
  const n = (k) => (typeof t[k] === 'number' ? t[k] : 0);
  return `Tiers: Confirmed ${n('Confirmed')} · Disputed ${n('Disputed')} · Contested ${n('Contested')} · Singleton ${n('Singleton')}`;
}

function stageSummary(run) {
  const stages = Array.isArray(run.stages) ? run.stages : [];
  return stages.length ? stages.map((s) => `${s.name}: ${s.status}`).join(' · ') : 'no stages recorded';
}

/**
 * @param {object} o {nonce, project, run, tally?, verdict?, chairText?}
 * @returns {string} the fold block (marker first line; no trailing newline)
 */
function buildFoldText(o) {
  // ⚠️ DE-ROT (F58): mirror formatFoldOutput's v4.0 §9 guard (src/headless.js:776).
  // Without it a missing nonce emits `[SIDECAR_FOLD:]`, which the hex-only marker
  // regex (src/utils/fold-marker.js:68) never parses — a silently unfoldable block.
  if (!o || !o.nonce) { throw new TypeError('buildFoldText requires a per-run nonce (v4.0 §9)'); }
  const run = o.run || {};
  const verdict = ok(o.verdict);
  const tally = ok(o.tally);
  const overall = verdict && verdict.overallVerdict ? verdict.overallVerdict : null;
  const tierCounts = (verdict && verdict.tierCounts) || (tally && tally.tierCounts) || null;
  const cost = run.usage && run.usage.cost ? run.usage.cost : null;

  const head = [
    buildFoldMarker(o.nonce),
    `Model: ${run.chair || 'unknown'}`,
    `Session: ${run.runId || 'unknown'}`,
    'Client: council-workspace',
    `CWD: ${o.project}`,
    'Mode: council',
    '---',
    `VERDICT: ${overall || 'none'}`,
  ];
  if (tierCounts) {
    head.push(tierLine(tierCounts));
  } else {
    head.push(`Run: ${run.status || 'unknown'} — ${stageSummary(run)}`);
  }
  head.push(`Cost: ${formatCost(cost)}${cost && cost.source ? ` (${cost.source})` : ''}`);

  const body = o.chairText && String(o.chairText).trim()
    ? stripFoldMarkers(String(o.chairText)).trim()
    : (tierCounts ? '(no chair output — tally summary above)' : '(pre-tally: stage summary above)');

  return `${head.join('\n')}\n${body}`;
}

module.exports = { buildFoldText };
```

### Task 7: `electron/preload-workspace.js` — minimal typed bridge

**Interfaces:**

- Produces: `window.amicusWorkspace = { invoke(channel, ...args) }` with the hardcoded 7-channel allowlist. NO `shell`, nothing else (spec §4.2 — contrast `preload-setup.js`, which exposes `openExternal`; report-opening here is a main-process concern via `workspace:open-report`).

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `electron/preload-workspace.js`**

```js
/**
 * Council Workspace Preload — minimal typed IPC bridge (v4.4 §4.2/§4.5).
 *
 * The workspace page loads via loadFile (a real file:// origin), so
 * contextBridge works under FULL sandbox — no data:-URL workaround needed
 * (docs/electron-testing.md Known Limitations; the setup wizard only works
 * because it runs sandbox:false, a posture this page must not inherit: it
 * renders untrusted model prose, the H9 surface).
 *
 * Exposes exactly one function. No openExternal bridge, no listeners.
 * Unknown channel → throw (preload-setup.js pattern).
 *
 * ⚠️ DE-ROT (F30): this header used to name the banned electron module (the
 * one preload-setup.js:9 imports for openExternal). Step 1 asserts
 * expect(SRC).not.toContain(...) against the RAW file source — comments
 * included — so that word here made Task 7 fail its own test. Never let the
 * literal token appear anywhere in this file. Step 4's "PASS (3 tests)" is
 * correct only with the reworded line above.
 */
const { contextBridge, ipcRenderer } = require('electron');

const ALLOWED_CHANNELS = [
  'workspace:list-runs',
  'workspace:get-run',
  'workspace:get-live',
  'workspace:read-artifact',
  'workspace:abort-run',
  'workspace:fold',
  'workspace:open-report',
];

contextBridge.exposeInMainWorld('amicusWorkspace', {
  /** Invoke an allowlisted workspace IPC channel. */
  invoke: (channel, ...args) => {
    if (!ALLOWED_CHANNELS.includes(channel)) {
      throw new Error(`IPC channel not allowed: ${channel}`);
    }
    return ipcRenderer.invoke(channel, ...args);
  },
});
```

**Files:**

**Interfaces:**

- Produces: `registerWorkspaceHandlers(getWindow, ctx) → {gate}` registering **all seven** channels (get-live/abort/fold arrive wired to deps that Tasks 14/16/17 finalize — their handler BODIES are complete here; what later tasks add is renderer UI + the real in-process deps remain the same defaults); `createFoldGate()` (two-flag latch, fold.js `folded`/`completed` pattern minus the fallback-destroy machinery). `ctx = { project, nonce, ipc?, gate?, deps? }`.
