```js
/**
 * Council Workspace — application state + wiring (v4.4 §5).
 * Historical rendering in this task; Task 15 adds the live poll loop into
 * the seams marked LIVE; Task 16/17 flip the Abort button + confirm dialog.
 */
(function () {
  'use strict';

  var invoke = function (ch) {
    var args = Array.prototype.slice.call(arguments, 1);
    return window.amicusWorkspace.invoke.apply(null, [ch].concat(args));
  };

  var state = {
    runId: null,
    detail: null,
    blind: false,
    labelByModel: {},
    listTimer: null,
    liveTimer: null, // LIVE (Task 15)
  };

  function labelOf(model) { return state.labelByModel[model] || null; }
  function isBlind() { return state.blind; }

  function $(id) { return document.getElementById(id); }

  // ---- run list ----------------------------------------------------------
  function refreshList() {
    return invoke('workspace:list-runs').then(function (rows) {
      window.AmicusRender.renderRunList($('run-list'), rows, state.runId, openRun);
    });
  }

  function startListLoop() {
    if (state.listTimer) { clearInterval(state.listTimer); }
    state.listTimer = setInterval(refreshList, 5000); // spec §4.3
    window.addEventListener('focus', refreshList);
  }

  // ---- run detail --------------------------------------------------------
  function openRun(runId) {
    state.runId = runId;
    return invoke('workspace:get-run', runId).then(function (detail) {
      state.detail = detail;
      renderDetail();
      refreshList();
    });
  }

  function renderDetail() {
    var d = state.detail;
    $('empty-state').hidden = true;
    $('run-view').hidden = false;
    var R = window.AmicusRender;

    if (!d || d.error || !d.derived) {
      $('run-title').textContent = (d && d.runId) || 'run';
      R.renderBanner($('banner'),
        'Run unreadable: ' + ((d && d.error) || (d && d.run && d.run.parseError) || 'unknown') +
        (d && d.runDir ? ' — ' + d.runDir : ''), '');
      return;
    }

    // blind default: computed ONCE per run-open from status (resolution 9)
    state.blind = window.AmicusLive.defaultBlind(d.run.status);
    $('blind-toggle').checked = state.blind;
    state.labelByModel = {};
    d.derived.names.forEach(function (p) { state.labelByModel[p.model] = p.label; });

    $('run-title').textContent = d.runId;
    R.renderHeaderChips($('run-chips'), d.run);
    R.renderGauge($('cost-gauge-fill'), $('cost-gauge-text'),
      d.derived.cost.costAmount, d.derived.cost.maxCost, d.derived.cost.totalDisplay);
    R.renderStageRail($('stage-rail'), d.derived.stageRail);

    renderBanners();
    renderSeatsPanel();
    renderMatrixPanel();
    renderVerdictPanel();
    R.renderCost($('cost-body'), d.derived.cost, state.blind, labelOf);
    wireLazyPanels();
    // LIVE (Task 15): startLiveLoop() when non-terminal
  }

  function renderBanners() {
    var d = state.detail;
    var R = window.AmicusRender;
    if (!d.derived.schemaSupported) {
      R.renderBanner($('banner'), 'This run was written by a different amicus version (schemaVersion ' +
        d.run.schemaVersion + ') — artifacts: ' + d.runDir, 'warn');
      return;
    }
    if (d.run.error) {
      R.renderBanner($('banner'), d.run.error, d.run.status === 'error' ? '' : 'warn');
      return;
    }
    R.renderBanner($('banner'), null);
  }

  function renderSeatsPanel() {
    var d = state.detail;
    var seats = window.AmicusLive.seatsFromRunStats(d.derived.cost.rows);
    window.AmicusRender.renderSeats($('seats-body'), seats, state.blind, labelOf);
  }

  function renderMatrixPanel() {
    window.AmicusMatrix.renderMatrix($('matrix-body'), state.detail.derived.matrix, drillIntoJudge);
  }

  function renderVerdictPanel() {
    var d = state.detail;
    var chairHost = window.AmicusMatrix.renderVerdict($('verdict-body'), d.derived.verdictPanel, {
      labelOf: labelOf,
      isBlind: isBlind,
      reportPresent: !!(d.artifacts['report.html'] && d.artifacts['report.html'].present),
      onFold: doFold,
      onOpenReport: function () { invoke('workspace:open-report', state.runId); },
    });
    if (d.artifacts['chair-output.md'] && d.artifacts['chair-output.md'].present) {
      invoke('workspace:read-artifact', state.runId, 'chair-output.md').then(function (res) {
        if (res.text) { window.AmicusMd.renderMdLite(chairHost, res.text, document); }
      });
    } else {
      chairHost.appendChild(window.AmicusRender.el('p', { className: 'empty-note' }, ['chair-output.md not written yet']));
    }
  }

  // ---- lazy prose panels (spec §5.2: load on first open, cache) ---------
  // ⚠️ DE-ROT (F61): keep this local mirror of the shipped sanitizeName (src/council/run-launch.js:92-94)
  // — pin the two with an equality assert in tests/electron/workspace-ui-static.test.js. Do NOT rebuild
  // the lists from Object.keys(state.detail.artifacts): filenames carry the SANITIZED id, which cannot be
  // inverted back to the model id that keys state.labelByModel, so blind labels would break.
  function sanitizeName(model) { return String(model).replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9._-]/g, '-'); }

  // ⚠️ DE-ROT (F09): as written this stacks a NEW listener on every renderDetail() — see the note
  // after wireLazyPanels(). Register the three listeners ONCE at boot and dispatch through a
  // module-level `loaders` map that renderDetail overwrites.
  function proseLoader(panelId, bodyId, files) {
    var panel = $(panelId);
    panel.addEventListener('toggle', function () {
      if (!panel.open || panel.dataset.loaded === '1') { return; }
      panel.dataset.loaded = '1';
      Promise.all(files().map(function (f) {
        return invoke('workspace:read-artifact', state.runId, f.name).then(function (res) {
          return { name: f.name, title: f.title, text: res.text || '', truncated: res.truncated, error: res.error };
        });
      })).then(function (sections) {
        window.AmicusRender.renderProseSections($(bodyId), sections.map(function (s) {
          return s.error ? { name: s.name, title: s.title, error: s.name + ' — ' + s.error } : s;
        }));
      });
    });
  }

  function wireLazyPanels() {
    ['reviews-panel', 'bundle-panel', 'judges-panel'].forEach(function (id) {
      var p = $(id);
      p.dataset.loaded = '0';
      p.open = false;
    });
    var bench = state.detail.run.bench || [];
    proseLoader('reviews-panel', 'reviews-body', function () {
      return bench.map(function (m) {
        var label = state.labelByModel[m];
        return { name: 'review-' + sanitizeName(m) + '.md', title: (state.blind && label ? label : m) };
      });
    });
    proseLoader('bundle-panel', 'bundle-body', function () {
      return [{ name: 'bundle-stage2.md', title: 'bundle-stage2.md (verbatim)' }];
    });
    proseLoader('judges-panel', 'judges-body', function () {
      return bench.map(function (m) {
        var label = state.labelByModel[m];
        return { name: 'judge-' + sanitizeName(m) + '.md', title: 'Judge ' + (state.blind && label ? label : m) };
      });
    });
  }
```

> ⚠️ **DE-ROT (F09) — listener leak that silently shows the WRONG run's prose.**
> `reviews-panel` / `bundle-panel` / `judges-panel` are **static nodes in index.html** — they are
> never recreated. But `wireLazyPanels()` is called from the bottom of `renderDetail()`, which runs
> on **every** `openRun()` *and* on every blind-toggle via `renderDetail_preserveBlind()`. Each call
> hands `proseLoader` a fresh closure over that render's `bench`, and `addEventListener` **stacks**
> rather than replaces. On the next `toggle`, listeners from every previous run fire in registration
> order — the oldest wins the `dataset.loaded = '1'` race.
>
> **Symptom:** open run A, then run B, expand "Stage-1 reviews" → the panel requests run **A**'s
> `review-<model>.md` names against run **B**'s runId. `readRunArtifact` validates against run B's
> bench-derived allowlist, so every request is rejected and the panel renders nothing but error
> rows. Run B's actual seats never appear. Nothing throws, and no test in this plan covers a
> run-to-run switch.
>
> **Fix:** register the three `toggle` listeners exactly once, at boot, outside `renderDetail`. Have
> each handler read `state.detail.run.bench` and `state.runId` **at fire time** instead of capturing
> them. Keep only the `panel.dataset.loaded = '0'; panel.open = false;` reset inside
> `renderDetail`. (Equivalent: one permanent listener per panel dispatching through a module-level
> `loaders = {}` map that `renderDetail` overwrites.) Add an e2e case in Task 18 that opens the
> complete fixture, switches to the degraded fixture, expands the reviews panel, and asserts the
> degraded run's own filenames are requested.

```js

  // ⚠️ DE-ROT (F38): on a `--debate` run a dispute cell can be a RE-VOTE, whose prose lives in
  // `revote-<sanitizeName(judge)>.md`, not `judge-*.md`. Decide per (judge, findingId) from
  // debate.json — never per run. Exact lookup in the note after this block.
  function drillIntoJudge(judgePair, findingId) {
    var panel = $('judges-panel');
    panel.open = true;
    var render = function () {
      var section = $('judges-body').querySelector('[data-artifact="judge-' + sanitizeName(judgePair.model) + '.md"]');
      if (section) {
        window.AmicusMatrix.highlightText(section, findingId);
        section.scrollIntoView({ block: 'start' });
      }
    };
    if (panel.dataset.loaded === '1') { render(); }
    else { setTimeout(render, 300); } // after the toggle-triggered load settles
  }

  // ---- verbs -------------------------------------------------------------
  function doFold() {
    var btn = document.getElementById('fold-btn');
    invoke('workspace:fold', state.runId).then(function (res) {
      if (res.ok) {
        btn.textContent = 'Folded ✓';
        btn.disabled = true;
        btn.title = res.already ? 'Already folded this session' : 'Fold written to the launching terminal';
      } else {
        btn.title = res.error || 'fold failed';
      }
    });
  }

  // ---- blind toggle + keyboard ------------------------------------------
  $('blind-toggle').addEventListener('change', function (e) {
    state.blind = !!e.target.checked;
    renderDetail_preserveBlind();
  });

  function renderDetail_preserveBlind() {
    var keep = state.blind;
    renderDetail();
    state.blind = keep;
    $('blind-toggle').checked = keep;
    renderSeatsPanel();
    renderMatrixPanel();
    renderVerdictPanel();
    window.AmicusRender.renderCost($('cost-body'), state.detail.derived.cost, state.blind, labelOf);
  }

  $('run-list').addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    var items = Array.prototype.slice.call($('run-list').querySelectorAll('li[data-run-id]'));
    if (!items.length) { return; }
    var idx = items.findIndex(function (li) { return li.dataset.runId === state.runId; });
    if (e.key === 'ArrowDown') { e.preventDefault(); openRun(items[Math.min(items.length - 1, idx + 1)].dataset.runId); }
    if (e.key === 'ArrowUp') { e.preventDefault(); openRun(items[Math.max(0, idx - 1)].dataset.runId); }
  });

  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    if (e.key === 'Escape') { $('dialog-abort').hidden = true; }
  });

  // ---- boot --------------------------------------------------------------
  var boot = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('runId');
  refreshList().then(function () {
    if (boot) { openRun(boot); }
  });
  startListLoop();

  window.AmicusApp = { isBlind: isBlind, openRun: openRun, state: state };
})();
```

> ⚠️ **DE-ROT (F38) — on a `--debate` run the dispute drill-in opens the wrong file.**
> Under `--debate` the FINAL `tally.json` is rebuilt from the debate's *replaced* adjudications
> (`src/council/run.js:272-283`), so a matrix `dispute` cell can be a **re-vote**. Its prose is
> `revote-<sanitizeName(judge)>.md` (`run-debate.js:241` → `run-launch.js:127-136`), while
> `judge-<judge>.md` is written once, back in Stage 2 (`run-stages.js:193`), and never updated.
> **Gate per `(judge, findingId)`, not per run.** A judge gets ONE re-vote leg and it covers only the
> ids that judge actually re-voted; every other dispute cell still belongs to `judge-*.md`, so
> `run.debate.outcome === 'ran'` is the wrong key. `debate.json` (`run-debate.js:257-262`) is the
> index — `revotes: [{judge, id, verdict, reason, applied}]` — and its `judge` is the bench ALIAS
> (`leg.modelInput || leg.model`, run-debate.js:136,158), the same key `revote-*.md` and
> `state.labelByModel` use, so no filename inversion is needed.
> **Do:** when `d.run.debate`, `invoke('workspace:read-artifact', runId, 'debate.json')` once per
> run-open and `JSON.parse` into `state.debate` (already allowlisted by ⚠️ DE-ROT F28; try/catch —
> an aborted or cost-ceiling-skipped debate never writes it). Then in `drillIntoJudge`:
> `var rv = ((state.debate && state.debate.revotes) || []).find(function (r) { return r.judge === judgePair.model && r.id === findingId; });`
> if `rv`, target `[data-artifact="revote-' + sanitizeName(judgePair.model) + '.md"]` and render
> `rv.reason` verbatim (`textContent`) above the highlighted prose; otherwise keep today's
> `judge-*.md` path. F28's debate panel must load the `revote-*.md` sections or this target will not exist.

```js
const { normalizeLive } = require('../../src/workspace/live-normalize');

describe('normalizeLive', () => {
  test('council composed doc: stages, active stage name, seats with live usage', () => {
    const doc = {
      // ⚠️ DE-ROT (F63): dropped `schemaVersion: 2` — the council live doc is UNVERSIONED.
      // buildCouncilStatusPayload builds a bare object literal and returns markLive(payload); it
      // never goes through stampEnvelope (src/mcp-server.js:209-212), which is the only thing that
      // stamps schemaVersion and is used only by the wave/single-session branches. `schemaVersion: 2`
      // lives on run.json (run.js:71). The schema banner is a run.json/RunDetail concern only.
      type: 'council-run', view: 'live', taskId: 'cccc3333', status: 'running',
      stages: [
        // ⚠️ DE-ROT (F40): dropped `startedAt: 'T0'` — the payload projects run.json's stages down to
        // exactly {name, status, waveId} (src/mcp-council-awareness.js:125-127), so the durable
        // timestamps never reach the live doc. See the F40 note in Task 15's stage-rail block for the
        // merge rule that keeps them on screen.
        { name: 'stage1', status: 'running', waveId: 'cccc3333-s1' },

      legs: [

          lastActivity: '5s ago', stalled: false,
          usage: { tokens: { input: 3000, output: 400 }, cost: { amount: 0.02, source: 'estimated' } } },
        { taskId: 'dddd0002', model: 'gpt', status: 'running', stalled: true },

    const m = normalizeLive(doc);
    expect(m.ok).toBe(true);
    expect(m.view).toBe('live');
    expect(m.runId).toBe('cccc3333');

    expect(m.terminal).toBe(false);
    expect(m.seats).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(m.seats[0]).toMatchObject({
      id: 'dddd0001', model: 'gemini', status: 'running', stage: 'receiving',

    });
    // A2 degradation: absent live fields → null (renderer paints —)

    expect(m.costDisplay).toBe('~$0.0600');  // ⚠️ DE-ROT (F24): formatCost uses 4dp under $1
    expect(m.costAmount).toBeCloseTo(0.06);

    expect(m.seats).toHaveLength(1);
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `src/workspace/live-normalize.js`**

```js
/**
 * Council Workspace — live-doc normalization (v4.4 §3 A1/A2/A4 seam).
 *
 * ONE defensive mapping from the v4.3 composed live doc (the amicus_status
 * rollup stamped view:'live') to the renderer's seat model. If the merged
 * composed-doc shape ever drifts, THIS file moves and nothing else does.
 * Liveness/staleness is passed through, never invented here (A4).
 */
'use strict';

const { formatCost } = require('../utils/pricing');
const { TERMINAL_STATUSES } = require('./run-detail');

function legRowsOf(doc) {
  if (Array.isArray(doc.legs)) { return doc.legs; }
  if (doc.wave && Array.isArray(doc.wave.legs)) { return doc.wave.legs; }
  return [];
}

function numOrNull(v) { return typeof v === 'number' ? v : null; }

function seatOf(leg) {
  const usage = leg.usage || null;
  const tokens = usage && usage.tokens ? usage.tokens : null;
  return {
    id: leg.taskId || leg.legId || null,
    model: leg.model || leg.modelInput || 'unknown',
    role: leg.role || null,
    status: leg.status || 'unknown',
    stage: leg.stage || leg.phase || null,
    messages: leg.messages === undefined ? null : leg.messages,
    tokensIn: tokens ? numOrNull(tokens.input) : null,
    tokensOut: tokens ? numOrNull(tokens.output) : null,
    costDisplay: usage && usage.cost ? formatCost(usage.cost) : null,
    lastActivity: leg.lastActivity || leg.latestActivity || null,
    latestPreview: leg.latestPreview || null,
    stalled: leg.stalled === true,
  };
}

/**
 * @param {object} doc composed live doc (amicus_status payload)
 * @returns {object} LiveModel (see plan Shared contracts); {ok:false, error?} on junk
 */
function normalizeLive(doc) {
  if (!doc || typeof doc !== 'object') { return { ok: false, error: 'no live doc' }; }
  const stages = Array.isArray(doc.stages) ? doc.stages : null;
  const active = stages ? (stages.find((s) => s.status === 'running') || null) : null;
  const status = doc.status || 'unknown';
  return {
    ok: true,
    view: doc.view || null,
    runId: doc.runId || doc.taskId || null,
    status,
    stageName: active ? active.name : (doc.stage || null),
    stages,
    seats: legRowsOf(doc).map(seatOf),
    // ⚠️ DE-ROT (F39): these two are ACTIVE-STAGE spend, not the run total.
    // buildCouncilStatusPayload rolls up only the legs of the currently-RUNNING stage's sub-waves
    // (mcp-council-awareness.js:136-146) and omits `usage` entirely until one of those legs flushes
    // progress.usage (:154), so the number under-reports and RESETS at each stage boundary; stages
    // with no `project` (tally, verdict) contribute nothing. Decision: keep the field, LABEL it
    // stage-scoped in the renderer (Task 15 suffixes the gauge text "(this stage)"). Do NOT try to
    // add it onto `derived.cost.costAmount` — that is null for the entire life of the live loop
    // (run-detail reads run.json's `usage`, which stays null until finalize(), run.js:98-102), so
    // "adding" is just a rename of the same stage figure. The only run total is the terminal one.
    costDisplay: doc.usage && doc.usage.cost ? formatCost(doc.usage.cost) : null,
    costAmount: doc.usage && doc.usage.cost && typeof doc.usage.cost.amount === 'number' ? doc.usage.cost.amount : null,
    flags: {
      crashed: doc.crashed === true,
      stalled: doc.stalled === true,
      stalledForSeconds: typeof doc.stalledForSeconds === 'number' ? doc.stalledForSeconds : null,
    },
    terminal: TERMINAL_STATUSES.includes(status),
  };
}

module.exports = { normalizeLive };
```

### Task 15: Renderer live loop — seats, dead-run banner, gauge top-up

**Files:**
- Modify: `electron/workspace-ui/workspace-app.js` (the `// LIVE (Task 15)` seams)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `workspace:get-live` → `LiveModel`; `AmicusLive.pollDelay`; `AmicusRender.renderSeats/renderBanner/renderGauge/renderStageRail`.
- Produces: renderer-driven polling per spec §4.3 (1.5 s visible+focused / 5 s otherwise; stop at terminal + one final `workspace:get-run`); per-seat live rows with `⏳ stalled` badges; red dead-run banner from data-layer flags only; live cost top-up on the gauge; stage rail refresh from live stages.

- [ ] **Step 1: Add the live-loop functions to `workspace-app.js`**

Insert after `renderVerdictPanel()` (before the lazy-panels section):

> ⚠️ **DE-ROT (F64) — removed a duplicated `startLiveLoop()` instruction.** This preamble originally
> also said to replace the `// LIVE (Task 15): startLiveLoop() when non-terminal` comment with a real
> call "as the last line of `renderDetail()`". But that comment already sits immediately after
> `wireLazyPanels();` — the exact insertion point the 3-line block further down in this step targets,
> whose third line is also `startLiveLoop();`. Applied literally, `renderDetail()` ended with two
> consecutive `startLiveLoop();` calls. **Only the 3-line block below applies, and it REPLACES the
> `// LIVE (Task 15)` comment line.**

```js
  // ---- live loop (spec §4.3; Task 15) -----------------------------------
  // ⚠️ DE-ROT (F42): stopLiveLoop can only clearTimeout a SCHEDULED tick — it cannot cancel an
  // invoke() already in flight, and the three visibilitychange/blur/focus listeners at the bottom of
  // this step re-enter startLiveLoop freely. Without a generation guard those forked chains outlive
  // stopLiveLoop, reassign state.liveTimer (orphaning the timer the event just scheduled), and paint
  // run A's legs into run B after openRun() swaps state.detail underneath them. The epoch + PER-TICK
  // runId pin below are mandatory, not optional hardening. Seed `liveEpoch: 0` in the `state` object.
  function stopLiveLoop() {
    state.liveEpoch = (state.liveEpoch || 0) + 1; // invalidates any in-flight tick
    if (state.liveTimer) { clearTimeout(state.liveTimer); state.liveTimer = null; }
  }

  function liveState(terminal) {
    return {
      terminal: terminal,
      visible: document.visibilityState === 'visible',
      focused: document.hasFocus(),
    };
  }

  function startLiveLoop() {
    stopLiveLoop();
    var d = state.detail;
    if (!d || !d.run || window.AmicusLive.TERMINAL_STATUSES.indexOf(d.run.status) !== -1) { return; }
    var epoch = state.liveEpoch; // F42: stopLiveLoop() above just bumped it; this chain owns it
    var tick = function () {
      // F42: pin the id PER TICK and SEND the pinned id — matching the reply against state.runId
      // while the request carried state.runId would still let a post-switch request hit run B.
      var runId = state.runId;
      invoke('workspace:get-live', runId).then(function (live) {
        if (epoch !== state.liveEpoch || runId !== state.runId) { return; } // stale chain: drop it
        applyLive(live);
        var terminal = !!(live && live.ok && live.terminal);
        if (terminal) {
          stopLiveLoop();
          // final durable snapshot refresh (spec §4.3)
          openRun(runId);
          return;
        }
        var delay = window.AmicusLive.pollDelay(liveState(false));
        state.liveTimer = setTimeout(tick, delay);
      }).catch(function () {
        // F42: a rejected invoke must not silently kill the loop — reschedule unless superseded.
        if (epoch !== state.liveEpoch || runId !== state.runId) { return; }
        state.liveTimer = setTimeout(tick, window.AmicusLive.pollDelay(liveState(false)));
      });
    };
    state.liveTimer = setTimeout(tick, 0);
  }

  function applyLive(live) {
    var R = window.AmicusRender;
    if (!live || !live.ok) {
      // A1 failure: keep last-known panels; flag the live layer only (spec §9)
      R.renderBanner($('banner'), 'live data unavailable' + (live && live.error ? ' — ' + live.error : ''), 'info');
      return;
    }
    if (live.seats.length) {
      R.renderSeats($('seats-body'), live.seats, state.blind, labelOf);
    }
    // F42: state.detail can be swapped/absent under a tick — never deref .derived unguarded.
    var derived = state.detail && state.detail.derived ? state.detail.derived : null;
    if (live.stages) {
      // ⚠️ DE-ROT (F41): the old `derived.stageRail.find(...) || {label: s.name}` lookup fell back to
      // the RAW stage name for every stage that STARTS AFTER run-open — which is most of them, since
      // state.detail is a frozen run-open snapshot and stageRail() only maps the stages already in
      // run.json. STAGE_LABELS lives main-process-side (src/workspace/run-detail.js, exported there)
      // and the sandboxed renderer cannot require it. **Task 12 must mirror STAGE_LABELS into
      // electron/workspace-ui/live-model.js beside TERMINAL_STATUSES (same "keep in sync" comment),
      // export it on window.AmicusLive, and assert in tests/workspace/live-model.test.js that the
      // mirrored table deep-equals run-detail.js's export.** Label from the mirror, not the snapshot.
      // ⚠️ DE-ROT (F40): live stage entries carry NO startedAt/completedAt, so reading them off `s`
      // wipes the durable times already on screen on the very first tick. Merge onto the run-open
      // snapshot row instead (a stage that begins mid-session simply shows no times until the
      // terminal openRun() refresh — status still updates every tick).
      R.renderStageRail($('stage-rail'), live.stages.map(function (s) {
        var prior = (derived ? derived.stageRail.find(function (r) { return r.name === s.name; }) : null) || {};
        return {
          name: s.name,
          label: window.AmicusLive.STAGE_LABELS[s.name] || s.name,
          status: s.status || 'pending',
          startedAt: prior.startedAt || null,
          completedAt: prior.completedAt || null,
        };
      }));
    }
    // ⚠️ DE-ROT (F39): guard on costAmount ALONE. costDisplay is raw formatCost output and can be
    // '?' (source 'unknown') or '—' (null cost); the old `|| live.costDisplay` arm let one of those
    // strings overwrite the durable total. And the value is ACTIVE-STAGE spend (see the F39 note in
    // Task 14) — it under-reports and resets between stages, so it is labelled, never sold as a run
    // total. The durable total returns on the terminal openRun() refresh.
    if (live.costAmount !== null) {
      R.renderGauge($('cost-gauge-fill'), $('cost-gauge-text'),
        live.costAmount, derived ? derived.cost.maxCost : null,
        (live.costDisplay || '—') + ' (this stage)');
    }
    // Dead-run banner: DATA-LAYER flags only (A4) — never GUI heuristics.
    if (live.flags.crashed || live.flags.stalled) {
      var mins = live.flags.stalledForSeconds ? Math.round(live.flags.stalledForSeconds / 60) : null;
      R.renderBanner($('banner'),
        'No leg activity' + (mins ? ' for ' + mins + 'm' : '') + ' — the run may be dead. Abort to reclaim it; everything on disk stays browsable.',
        '');
      $('abort-btn').hidden = false; // the remedy ships beside the diagnosis (Task 16 wires it)
    } else if ($('banner').classList.contains('info')) {
      R.renderBanner($('banner'), null);
    }
  }
```

And extend the focus/visibility reactivity + cleanup at the bottom of the file (before the `window.AmicusApp` export):

```js
  // ⚠️ DE-ROT (F42): these three re-enter startLiveLoop on every focus/blur/visibility flip. That is
  // only safe because startLiveLoop() calls stopLiveLoop() first, which BUMPS state.liveEpoch — any
  // tick already awaiting invoke() sees the mismatch and drops instead of rescheduling itself.
  // Without the epoch bump each flip forks a second poll chain that no stop can reach.
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', function () {
    if (state.liveTimer) { startLiveLoop(); }
  });
  window.addEventListener('blur', function () { if (state.liveTimer) { startLiveLoop(); } });
  window.addEventListener('focus', function () { if (state.liveTimer) { startLiveLoop(); } });
```

```bash
WSPROJ=$(node -e "
const fs=require('fs'),os=require('os'),path=require('path');
const proj=fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(),'ws-live-'));
const runDir=path.join(proj,'run-cccc3333');
copyRunFixture(path.resolve('tests','fixtures','council-run-live'), runDir);  // ⚠️ DE-ROT (F16): a plain cpSync leaves the __RUNDIR__ sentinel unresolved
const s=path.join(proj,'.claude','amicus_sessions');fs.mkdirSync(s,{recursive:true});
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(s,'council-cccc3333.json'),JSON.stringify({runId:'cccc3333',runDir}));
console.log(proj);")
AMICUS_MODE=council-workspace AMICUS_PROJECT="$WSPROJ" AMICUS_RUN_ID=cccc3333 "$(node -e "console.log(require('electron'))")" electron/main.js &
sleep 6
# ⚠️ DE-ROT (F43): NEVER interpolate $WSPROJ into the node -e source. mkdtempSync returns a Windows
# backslash path (C:\Users\sendt\AppData\Local\Temp\ws-live-XXXX); inside a JS string literal \U \s
# \A \L \T are unrecognized escapes and collapse to the bare character, so the join always ENOENTs
# and this step can never pass on Windows. Pass the dir through the ENVIRONMENT instead — and apply
# the same rule anywhere else a real path is fed to `node -e`.
WSPROJ="$WSPROJ" node -e "
const fs=require('fs'),path=require('path');
const p=path.join(process.env.WSPROJ,'run-cccc3333','.claude','amicus_sessions','dddd0001','progress.json');
const d=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(p,'utf-8'));
d.stage='complete';d.messages=9;d.latest='Complete';
fs.writeFileSync(p,JSON.stringify(d));
console.log('mutated');"
```

Expected: the window opens on `cccc3333` with the running pulse chip, blind toggle ON (labels shown in seats), three seat rows with live stage/messages/tokens; within ~3 s of the mutation the gemini row (shown as `Review A`) reads `complete` / `9` msgs. Close; exit 0.

### Task 16: Abort verb — confirm dialog wired to the engine's abort path

**Files:**

**Interfaces:**

- Produces: confirm-gated Abort per spec §8: header button (visible on non-terminal runs since Task 15) → dialog → on confirm invoke abort → refresh the run.

> ⚠️ **DE-ROT (F05 + F45).** Two corrections to this task:
> 1. **Target file:** this code goes in **`workspace-verbs.js`** (created in Task 13), not
>    `workspace-app.js`. Adding these ~21 lines to `workspace-app.js` re-blows the 300-line gate that
>    Task 13's split exists to prevent, and this task's gate carries no escape hatch.
> 2. **This task ships with no real verification.** Its only gate is
>    `workspace-ui-static.test.js` (script order + no-innerHTML) plus the size check — neither of
>    which this diff can fail. That is false confidence on the product's only destructive verb. Add
>    a **sixth e2e case in Task 18** against the live fixture: click Abort → confirm → assert the
>    status chip flips to `aborted`, the button hides, and the poll stops. (A jsdom unit test is
>    *not* an option: `jest.config.js` sets `testEnvironment: 'node'` and the plan's Global
>    Constraints forbid new devDependencies — there is no jsdom in the tree.)

> ⚠️ **DE-ROT (F44) — `workspace:abort-run` is the one channel that writes; "the engine's abort path" is a misnomer.**
> `handlers.amicus_abort` is a plain in-process function, so the **Electron workspace process itself**
> mutates the run dir. For a council `runId`, `readMetadata` misses (council runs never create a session
> dir — `src/mcp-council-run.js:116-126` writes only `run.json` + a pointer), so it falls through to
> `abortCouncilRun` (`src/mcp-server.js:994` → `src/mcp-council-awareness.js:206-227`), which performs
> v4.0's marker-first abort: one `run.json` status checkpoint (abort-wins merge,
> `src/council/run-state.js:72-92`), abort markers on in-flight wave/leg `metadata.json`, then a
> best-effort SIGTERM of the engine pid after the 5 s grace.
> **Restate the invariant as:** the workspace is read-only against run directories **except
> `workspace:abort-run`**. Scope any read-only-guard test to exclude the abort channel explicitly, and
> name `src/mcp-council-awareness.js` — not "the engine" — as the code that writes. (Same correction is
> owed in Global Constraints and in the Task 19 doc bullet; both live outside this task.)

- [ ] **Step 1: Wire the dialog in `workspace-verbs.js`** (add to the verbs section, after `doFold`)

```js
  function openAbortDialog() {
    $('dialog-abort').hidden = false;
    $('dialog-abort-cancel').focus();
  }

  $('abort-btn').addEventListener('click', openAbortDialog);
  $('dialog-abort-cancel').addEventListener('click', function () { $('dialog-abort').hidden = true; });
  $('dialog-abort-confirm').addEventListener('click', function () {
    var btn = $('dialog-abort-confirm');
    btn.disabled = true;
    invoke('workspace:abort-run', state.runId).then(function (res) {
      btn.disabled = false;
      $('dialog-abort').hidden = true;
      if (!res.ok) {
        window.AmicusRender.renderBanner($('banner'), 'Abort failed: ' + (res.error || res.detail || 'unknown'), '');
        return;
      }
      stopLiveLoop();
      openRun(state.runId); // re-read: status flips to aborted, grey chip, no live poll
    });
  });
```

(The Esc-to-dismiss handler already exists from Task 13; the dialog's Cancel receives focus so Esc/Enter both behave.)

**Files:**

- Produces: `launchWorkspaceWindow({project, runId}, deps?) → Promise<{code, error?}>` — spawns Electron in `council-workspace` mode, **relays child stdout live** (the fold channel) to our stdout, propagates the exit code. CLI: `amicus watch <councilRunId> --ui` and bare `amicus watch --ui` (run-list landing); `--ui --json` → exit 1 with a stderr hint (interactive-only envelope exclusion).

Create `tests/workspace/launcher.test.js`:

```js
'use strict';

const { EventEmitter } = require('events');

const { launchWorkspaceWindow } = require('../../src/sidecar/workspace-window');

function fakeChild() {
  const child = new EventEmitter();
  child.stdout = new EventEmitter();
  child.stdout.setEncoding = () => {};
  child.stderr = new EventEmitter();
  child.stderr.setEncoding = () => {};
  return child;
}

describe('launchWorkspaceWindow', () => {
  test('spawns electron main.js with the workspace env contract and relays stdout live', async () => {
    const child = fakeChild();
    const spawn = jest.fn(() => child);
    const writes = [];
    const stdoutWrite = jest.spyOn(process.stdout, 'write').mockImplementation((c) => { writes.push(String(c)); return true; });

    const promise = launchWorkspaceWindow({ project: 'C:\\proj', runId: 'aaaa1111' }, {
      ensureElectron: async () => ({ ok: true, path: 'C:\\electron.exe' }),
      spawn,
      nonce: 'cafef00dcafef00d',
    });
    child.stdout.emit('data', 'FOLD LINE 1\n');
    child.emit('close', 0);
    const res = await promise;
    stdoutWrite.mockRestore();

    expect(res.code).toBe(0);
    expect(writes).toContain('FOLD LINE 1\n');
    const [bin, args, opts] = spawn.mock.calls[0];
    expect(bin).toBe('C:\\electron.exe');
    expect(args[args.length - 1]).toMatch(/electron[\\/]+main\.js$/);
    expect(opts.env).toMatchObject({
      AMICUS_MODE: 'council-workspace',
      AMICUS_PROJECT: 'C:\\proj',
      AMICUS_RUN_ID: 'aaaa1111',
      AMICUS_FOLD_NONCE: 'cafef00dcafef00d',
    });
    expect(opts.stdio).toEqual(['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe']);
  });

  test('empty runId launches the run-list landing; debug port adds the CDP arg', async () => {
    const child = fakeChild();
    const spawn = jest.fn(() => child);
    process.env.AMICUS_DEBUG_PORT = '9225';
    const promise = launchWorkspaceWindow({ project: '/p', runId: '' }, {
      ensureElectron: async () => ({ ok: true, path: 'electron' }), spawn,
    });
    child.emit('close', 0);
    await promise;
    delete process.env.AMICUS_DEBUG_PORT;
    const [, args, opts] = spawn.mock.calls[0];
    expect(args[0]).toBe('--remote-debugging-port=9225');
    expect(opts.env.AMICUS_RUN_ID).toBe('');
    expect(opts.env.AMICUS_FOLD_NONCE).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f]{16}$/);
  });

  test('ensureElectron failure and spawn error resolve {code:1, error}', async () => {
    const res = await launchWorkspaceWindow({ project: '/p' }, { ensureElectron: async () => ({ ok: false, reason: 'no electron' }) });
    expect(res).toEqual({ code: 1, error: 'no electron' });

    const child = fakeChild();
    const promise = launchWorkspaceWindow({ project: '/p' }, {
      ensureElectron: async () => ({ ok: true, path: 'x' }),
      spawn: () => child,
    });
    child.emit('error', new Error('ENOENT'));
    const res2 = await promise;
    expect(res2.code).toBe(1);
    expect(res2.error).toMatch(/ENOENT/);
  });
});
```

Create `tests/workspace/watch-ui.test.js`:

```js
'use strict';

describe('amicus watch --ui branch', () => {
  afterEach(() => { jest.resetModules(); jest.dontMock('../../src/sidecar/workspace-window'); });

  test('--ui routes to the launcher with cwd project + positional runId and returns its code', async () => {
    const launch = jest.fn(async () => ({ code: 0 }));
    jest.doMock('../../src/sidecar/workspace-window', () => ({ launchWorkspaceWindow: launch }));
    const { handleWatch } = require('../../src/cli-handlers-watch');
    const code = await handleWatch({ _: ['watch', 'aaaa1111'], ui: true, cwd: 'C:\\proj' });
    expect(code).toBe(0);
    expect(launch).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ project: 'C:\\proj', runId: 'aaaa1111' });
  });

  test('bare --ui opens the run list (empty runId)', async () => {
    const launch = jest.fn(async () => ({ code: 0 }));
    jest.doMock('../../src/sidecar/workspace-window', () => ({ launchWorkspaceWindow: launch }));
    const { handleWatch } = require('../../src/cli-handlers-watch');
    await handleWatch({ _: ['watch'], ui: true, cwd: '/p' });
    expect(launch).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ project: '/p', runId: '' });
  });

  test('--ui --json is refused as interactive-only (exit 1, stderr hint)', async () => {
    const { handleWatch } = require('../../src/cli-handlers-watch');
    const err = jest.spyOn(process.stderr, 'write').mockImplementation(() => true);
    const code = await handleWatch({ _: ['watch', 'aaaa1111'], ui: true, json: true });
    err.mockRestore();
    expect(code).toBe(1);
    expect(err.mock.calls.map((c) => c[0]).join('')).toMatch(/interactive-only/);
  });

  test('cli.js parses --ui as a boolean flag and documents it', () => {

    expect(args.ui).toBe(true);
    expect(args._[1]).toBe('aaaa1111');
    expect(getUsage('watch')).toContain('--ui');
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `src/sidecar/workspace-window.js`**

```js
/**
 * Council Workspace launcher (v4.4 §4.3/§4.4) — setup-window.js pattern:
 * ensureElectron() (the one place provisioning is allowed, #55) → spawn
 * electron/main.js in council-workspace mode. Unlike setup (which buffers),
 * this RELAYS child stdout live — the nonced fold block must reach the
 * launching terminal's command output verbatim. Exit code propagates
 * (0 on fold-then-close and on plain close).
 */
'use strict';

const { spawn } = require('child_process');
const path = require('path');
const { logger } = require('../utils/logger');
const { getElectronPath } = require('./interactive-process');
const { ensureElectron } = require('./electron-ensure');
const { generateFoldNonce } = require('../utils/fold-marker');

/**
 * @param {{project: string, runId?: string}} opts
 * @param {{ensureElectron?: Function, spawn?: Function, nonce?: string}} [deps] test injection
 * @returns {Promise<{code: number, error?: string}>}
 */
async function launchWorkspaceWindow({ project, runId = '' }, deps = {}) {
  const ensure = deps.ensureElectron || ensureElectron;
  const spawnFn = deps.spawn || spawn;
  const ensured = await ensure();
  if (!ensured.ok) {
    return { code: 1, error: ensured.reason || 'Electron not installed' };
  }
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const electronPath = ensured.path || getElectronPath();
    const mainPath = path.join(__dirname, '..', '..', 'electron', 'main.js');
    const env = {
      ...process.env,
      AMICUS_MODE: 'council-workspace',
      AMICUS_PROJECT: project,
      AMICUS_RUN_ID: runId || '',
      AMICUS_FOLD_NONCE: deps.nonce || generateFoldNonce(),
    };
    const debugPort = process.env.AMICUS_DEBUG_PORT;
    const args = debugPort ? [`--remote-debugging-port=${debugPort}`, mainPath] : [mainPath];
    logger.info('Launching council workspace', { runId: runId || '(run list)', debugPort: debugPort || 'disabled' });

    const proc = spawnFn(electronPath, args, { env, stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'] });

    proc.stdout.setEncoding('utf-8');
    proc.stdout.on('data', (chunk) => { process.stdout.write(chunk); }); // fold relay — live, verbatim
    proc.stderr.setEncoding('utf-8');
    proc.stderr.on('data', (chunk) => { logger.debug('Workspace stderr', { data: String(chunk).trim() }); });

    proc.on('error', (err) => {
      logger.error('Workspace failed to spawn', { error: err.message });
      resolve({ code: 1, error: `Failed to start workspace: ${err.message}` });
    });
    proc.on('close', (code) => {
      logger.info('Workspace closed', { code });
      resolve({ code: code === null ? 1 : code });
    });
  });
}

module.exports = { launchWorkspaceWindow };
```

- Produces: the spec §10 e2e asserts: run list chips; tier-colored matrix + basis counts; blind toggle swaps a known model name; verdict line + street-cred rows; fold → spawned stdout contains the nonced marker + VERDICT; degraded run renders "no chair verdict"; live-update within 2 poll intervals.

- [ ] **Step 1: Add the workspace factory to `tests/helpers/cdp-client.js`** (after the `content` factory, before the closing brace of the class)

```js
  /**
   * Factory: connect to the Council Workspace target (file:// URL — the
   * workspace is the only file:// page; toolbar=data:, content=http).
   * @param {number} port - CDP debug port
   * @param {number} timeoutMs - Max wait time
   * @returns {Promise<CdpClient>}
   */
  static async workspace(port = 9223, timeoutMs = 15000) {
    const cdp = new CdpClient(port);
    const start = Date.now();
    while (Date.now() - start < timeoutMs) {
      try {
        const target = await cdp.findTarget(t => t.type === 'page' && t.url && t.url.startsWith('file://'));
        if (target) { await cdp.connect(target.id); return cdp; }
      } catch { /* not ready */ }
      await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 500));
    }
    throw new Error(`Workspace target not found after ${timeoutMs}ms`);
  }
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Create `tests/electron-workspace-e2e.integration.test.js`**

> ⚠️ **DE-ROT (F06) — as written this suite turns CI red on every push.** The plan assumes the
> `.integration.test.js` suffix keeps a file out of CI. That stopped being true on **2026-07-21, two

>

>
> **Do both:** copy the `ensureDisplay()` helper verbatim into this suite, call it in `beforeAll`,
> pass `DISPLAY` into `launchWorkspace()`'s env, **and** keep the `HAS_DISPLAY` gate added to

> Expected: the workspace suite reports **skipped** (not failed) on a machine without a display.

```js
/**
 * Council Workspace E2E (CDP) — fixture-driven, ZERO model calls / zero spend.
 * Spawns the real Electron shell in council-workspace mode against a temp
 * project seeded with the checked-in synthetic run dirs, asserts DOM state
 * over CDP, mutates the live fixture's progress.json mid-test, and captures
 * the fold block from the spawned process stdout.
 *
 * Skips when the electron binary is unavailable (toolbar-e2e pattern).
 * Port 9225: 9223 is the manual/docs port, 9224 belongs to the toolbar suite.
 */
'use strict';

const { spawn } = require('child_process');
const fs = require('fs');
const os = require('os');
const path = require('path');
const { CdpClient } = require('./helpers/cdp-client');

const ELECTRON_BIN = (() => { try { return require('electron'); } catch { return null; } })();
const ELECTRON_MAIN = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'electron', 'main.js');
const CDP_PORT = 9225;
const FX = path.join(__dirname, 'fixtures');
const NONCE = 'cafef00dcafef00d';

// ⚠️ DE-ROT (F06): ELECTRON_BIN alone is NOT enough — see the note below this block.
const HAS_DISPLAY = process.platform !== 'linux' || !!process.env.DISPLAY;
const describeE2E = (ELECTRON_BIN && HAS_DISPLAY) ? describe : describe.skip;

function seedTempProject() {
  const proj = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'ws-e2e-'));
  const sessions = path.join(proj, '.claude', 'amicus_sessions');
  fs.mkdirSync(sessions, { recursive: true });
  // live fixture is COPIED (the test mutates its progress.json)
  const liveDir = path.join(proj, 'run-cccc3333');
  // ⚠️ DE-ROT (F16): must be copyRunFixture — a plain cpSync leaves stages[].project as the
  // literal "__RUNDIR__" sentinel, so the leg rollup resolves against a nonexistent dir and the
  // live seats panel stays empty for the whole e2e.
  copyRunFixture(path.join(FX, 'council-run-live'), liveDir);
  const entries = {
    aaaa1111: path.join(FX, 'council-run-complete'),
    bbbb2222: path.join(FX, 'council-run-degraded'),
    cccc3333: liveDir,
  };
  for (const [runId, runDir] of Object.entries(entries)) {
    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(sessions, `council-${runId}.json`), JSON.stringify({ runId, runDir }));
  }
  return { proj, liveDir };
}

function launchWorkspace(proj, runId) {
  const child = spawn(ELECTRON_BIN, [`--remote-debugging-port=${CDP_PORT}`, ELECTRON_MAIN], {
    env: {
      ...process.env,
      AMICUS_MODE: 'council-workspace',
      AMICUS_PROJECT: proj,
      AMICUS_RUN_ID: runId || '',
      AMICUS_FOLD_NONCE: NONCE,
      AMICUS_HEADLESS_TEST: '1',
    },
    stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'],
  });
  let stdout = '';
  child.stdout.setEncoding('utf-8');
  child.stdout.on('data', (c) => { stdout += c; });
  child.stderr.setEncoding('utf-8');
  child.stderr.on('data', () => {});
  return { child, getStdout: () => stdout };
}

function kill(child) {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    if (!child || child.exitCode !== null) { resolve(); return; }
    child.on('close', resolve);
    child.kill('SIGTERM');
    setTimeout(() => { try { child.kill('SIGKILL'); } catch { /* gone */ } resolve(); }, 4000);
  });
}

describeE2E('council workspace e2e (CDP)', () => {
  jest.setTimeout(120000);

  let proj;
  let liveDir;

  beforeAll(() => { ({ proj, liveDir } = seedTempProject()); });

  test('run list renders all three fixtures with status chips', async () => {
    const { child } = launchWorkspace(proj, '');
    const cdp = await CdpClient.workspace(CDP_PORT);
    try {
      await cdp.waitForSelector('#run-list li');
      const rows = await cdp.evaluate(`
        Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#run-list li')).map(li => ({
          id: li.querySelector('.mono')?.textContent,
          chip: li.querySelector('.chip')?.textContent || null,
        }))`);
      expect(rows.map((r) => r.id)).toEqual(['cccc3333', 'aaaa1111', 'bbbb2222']);
      expect(rows[1].chip).toBe('complete');
      expect(rows[2].chip).toBe('partial');
    } finally { cdp.close(); await kill(child); }
  });

  test('complete run: tier-colored matrix, basis counts, verdict, street-cred, blind toggle', async () => {
    const { child } = launchWorkspace(proj, 'aaaa1111');
    const cdp = await CdpClient.workspace(CDP_PORT);
    try {
      await cdp.waitForSelector('#matrix-body table');
      const matrix = await cdp.evaluate(`
        Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#matrix-body tbody tr')).map(tr => ({
          cls: tr.className,
          id: tr.dataset.findingId,
          last: tr.lastElementChild.textContent,
        }))`);
      expect(matrix).toHaveLength(4);
      expect(matrix.find((r) => r.id === 'A1')).toMatchObject({ cls: 'tier-Confirmed', last: '2/0/0' });
      expect(matrix.find((r) => r.id === 'B1').cls).toBe('tier-Disputed');

      const verdict = await cdp.evaluate(`document.querySelector('#verdict-body .chip').textContent`);
      expect(verdict).toBe('VERDICT: Fix these first');
      const credRows = await cdp.evaluate(`document.querySelectorAll('#verdict-body table tbody tr').length`);
      expect(credRows).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3);

      // blind toggle: terminal default OFF → model names visible; toggling swaps to labels
      const before = await cdp.evaluate(`document.querySelector('#matrix-body thead').textContent`);
      expect(before).toContain('gemini');
      await cdp.evaluate(`document.getElementById('blind-toggle').click()`);
      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 300));
      const after = await cdp.evaluate(`document.querySelector('#matrix-body thead').textContent`);
      expect(after).toContain('Review A');
      expect(after).not.toContain('gemini');
    } finally { cdp.close(); await kill(child); }
  });

  test('fold click emits the nonced marker + VERDICT on the spawned process stdout', async () => {
    const { child, getStdout } = launchWorkspace(proj, 'aaaa1111');
    const cdp = await CdpClient.workspace(CDP_PORT);
    try {
      await cdp.waitForSelector('#fold-btn');
      await cdp.evaluate(`document.getElementById('fold-btn').click()`);
      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1500));
      const out = getStdout();
      expect(out).toContain('[SIDECAR_FOLD:' + NONCE + ']');
      expect(out).toContain('VERDICT: Fix these first');
      expect(out).toContain('Client: council-workspace');
      expect(out).not.toContain('deadbeefdeadbeef'); // planted marker stripped
      const btn = await cdp.evaluate(`document.getElementById('fold-btn').textContent`);
      expect(btn).toBe('Folded ✓');
    } finally { cdp.close(); await kill(child); }
  });

  test('degraded run renders "no chair verdict" + the engine reason', async () => {
    const { child } = launchWorkspace(proj, 'bbbb2222');
    const cdp = await CdpClient.workspace(CDP_PORT);
    try {
      await cdp.waitForSelector('#verdict-body .chip');
      const chip = await cdp.evaluate(`document.querySelector('#verdict-body .chip').textContent`);
      expect(chip).toBe('no chair verdict');
      const banner = await cdp.evaluate(`document.getElementById('banner').textContent`);
      expect(banner).toContain('chair failed after retry and fallback promotion');
      const chairNote = await cdp.evaluate(`document.getElementById('verdict-body').textContent`);
      expect(chairNote).toContain('chair-output.md not written yet');
    } finally { cdp.close(); await kill(child); }
  });

  test('live run: progress.json mutation reflects in the seat row within 2 poll intervals', async () => {
    const { child } = launchWorkspace(proj, 'cccc3333');
    const cdp = await CdpClient.workspace(CDP_PORT);
    try {
      await cdp.waitForSelector('#seats-body tr');
      // blind default ON for live runs: gemini shows as Review A
      const names = await cdp.evaluate(`Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#seats-body tr td:first-child')).map(td => td.textContent)`);
      expect(names).toContain('Review A');

      const progressPath = path.join(liveDir, '.claude', 'amicus_sessions', 'dddd0001', 'progress.json');
      const progress = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(progressPath, 'utf-8'));
      progress.stage = 'complete';
      progress.messagesReceived = 9;  // ⚠️ DE-ROT (F23 knock-on): readProgress never reads `messages`
      fs.writeFileSync(progressPath, JSON.stringify(progress));

      // ⚠️ DE-ROT (F47): was `+ 4000; // 2 × 1.5s poll + slack` — wrong cadence. Task 12's
      // pollDelay returns 1500 ONLY for {visible:true, focused:true}; every other combo is 5000.
      // Task 15 derives those live (visibilityState/hasFocus) and Task 9 never calls win.show()
      // under AMICUS_HEADLESS_TEST — so this window is never visible/focused and polls at 5s.
      const deadline = Date.now() + 12000; // 2 × 5s poll (headless window is never visible/focused) + slack
      let seen = false;
      while (Date.now() < deadline && !seen) {
        const rows = await cdp.evaluate(`Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#seats-body tr')).map(tr => tr.textContent)`);
        seen = rows.some((r) => r.includes('9'));
        if (!seen) { await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 400)); }
      }
      expect(seen).toBe(true);
    } finally { cdp.close(); await kill(child); }
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 1: `docs/council.md` — append the workspace section**

```markdown
## Council Workspace (GUI)

Watch a council think — not just tail a log:

    amicus watch <councilRunId> --ui     # open one run
    amicus watch --ui                    # open the run list for this project

One window per launch: run list (current project's council runs), stage rail,
live per-seat progress (status / stage / messages / tokens / cost, 1.5–2 s
poll of the v4.3 data layer), the verbatim anonymized Stage-2 packet, the
adjudication matrix (tier-colored, `a/d/n` basis counts, thin-confidence and
override badges, ✓/✗/– symbols), dissent drill-in (click a ✗ cell to open
that judge's prose with the finding id highlighted — rationale lives in
prose; the Stage-2 contract has no structured reason field), chair verdict +
street-cred, and cost-by-seat with the `--max-cost` ceiling gauge.

- **Historical runs** render entirely from the run directory — open any old
  `council-<runId>` at any time.
- **Blind mode** (toggle, top right): labels (`Review A`…) instead of model
  names — ON by default while a run is live, OFF for finished runs. Reading
  aid, not security: the label map is in `run.json` on your disk.
- **Two verbs only:** **Abort** (confirm-gated; the engine's own
  council-aware abort) and **Fold** (writes the nonced `[SIDECAR_FOLD:…]`
  block + chair verdict to the launching terminal — no model call, instant).
  Closing the window never auto-folds; reopen and fold any time.
- The workspace never writes into the run directory, and `--ui` is
  interactive-only (no `--json`).
- Degraded states are rendered honestly: dead-run banner (data-layer
  staleness), partial-wave badges, "no chair verdict" with the engine's
  reason, schema-mismatch banner for runs from other amicus versions.
```

## Self-review (writing-plans checklist, performed 2026-07-19)

**Spec coverage sweep:** §4.1 window model → Task 9; §4.2 sandbox/CSP/tokens/md-lite → Tasks 7/9/10/11; §4.3 process model + poll cadences → Tasks 8/15/17; §4.4 CLI entry → Task 17; §4.5 all 7 channels + reserved `live-update` → Tasks 7/8; §5.1 run list → Tasks 2/12/13; §5.2 all nine panels → Tasks 3/5/12/13/15 (header, stage rail, seats, reviews, peer packet, matrix, dissent drill-in w/ honesty note, verdict incl. degraded fold-button hosting, cost+gauge); §5.3 design system/a11y → Task 11 (token-only CSS test, focus-visible, reduced-motion, symbols+aria); §5.4 caps → MATRIX_ROW_CAP 500 + keyed seat rows; §6 anonymity → read-only invariant (Global Constraints + guard tests), blind defaults (Task 13/15), verbatim bundle panel, chair-packet browsable via artifact list; §7 fold → Tasks 6/8/13 (+ close latch Task 9); §8 verbs → Tasks 8/16 (abort) + fold; §9 degraded table → every row mapped (error rows Task 2, parseError/schema/missing-artifact Task 3, dead-run/live-unavailable Task 15, quorum/partial via fixtures + banners, ceiling via gauge `over` + run.error banner, Electron-missing via launcher error); §10 testing → Tasks 1–18; §11 rollout phasing preserved (historical first); §12 deferred items stay out.
**Placeholder scan:** no TBD/TODO/"similar to"; every code step carries complete code; Task 3's forward dependency is handled by explicit step ordering (red test → execute Tasks 4–5 → implement), not placeholders.
**Type consistency:** `RunRow`/`RunDetail`/`MatrixModel`/`LiveModel`/`Seat` shapes defined once in Shared contracts and used byte-consistently in Tasks 2/3/5/8/12/13/14/15/18; `readPointer` lives in run-scan (no cycle); `seatCells` column order matches the seats `<thead>` and the e2e asserts; fixture numbers (costs `$0.4321`/`~$0.31`, tier counts 1/1/1/1, basis `2/0/0`) reused identically in unit + e2e asserts.
